# NFL Fantasy / Dynasty — Story Prompts (per-vertical catalog)

**Story-ID scheme: `NF<n>` (NFL-Fantasy — parallel to MLB's `F`; distinct from NFL-betting `N…`).** Moved out of the NFL betting catalog (was `N1.3`) 2026-07-22.
**Parent roadmap:** `nfl_fantasy_roadmap.md` · **Betting sibling (builds the SHARED player-week core):** `../nfl_story_prompts.md` (N1.2) · **Rookie feeder:** NCAAF-`P1A` (done) · **Reference:** MLB `../../../baseball/fantasy/` (F-series) + the NFL N-series conventions.
**Conventions:** `uv run python`; DuckDB/S3-native (SF-free); do NOT `git commit`/`push`; honest-analytics framing — this is a PROJECTION product, NO betting-edge claim (`best_alpha` N/A). §0.5 where a predictive model is selected.
🔗 **THE SHARED CORE:** the per-player-week posterior-predictive projection is built in **N1.2 (props)** and consumed here — build once, don't duplicate. NF1 refines it into fantasy points; if NF1 runs before N1.2, IT builds the shared core (coordinate).

All findings measured live vs the NFL Delta lake + nflverse release Parquet (Appendix A was a hypothesis to VERIFY — which mattered).**
✅ **NF-W0 VERDICT (2026-08-05) — READ BEFORE NF-W1 / NF-W0a:**
 💰 **PAID CHARTING = NO-GO, $0.** 7 of the charting families are FREE in nflverse for NFL (coverage shell/man-zone via `pbp_participation` 100% 2023–25; box counts 99.98%; pressure `was_pressure`+PFR; separation/cushion/YAC-oe/aDOT via NGS; personnel via `offense_personnel`+FTN). Only 3 families genuinely gated (true route participation→TPRR/YPRR · first-read target share · individual OL grades), each with a free substitute. ⛔ do NOT buy for V1/V2 — re-evaluate ONLY after V1 quantifies the dropback-proxy shortfall (buying first makes the incremental-CRPS comparison uninterpretable; charting vendors supply current data w/o historical release-time snapshots so value must be proven prospectively anyway). ⚠️ NO vendor contacted — all v3 costs are planning estimates, not quotes.
 🪤 **APPENDIX A WRONG BOTH WAYS:** (a) `pbp_participation.route` is NOT route participation — it's the targeted receiver's route only (14 names, ~41% of plays), NO per-player attribution ⇒ routes-run/TPRR/YPRR unavailable free. Free substitute = `offense_players` (100% non-null 2016–25: per-player dropback/RZ/GL/3rd-down/2-min participation) — an UPPER BOUND on routes (a blocking TE is on the field, ran no route), ⛔ NEVER label it "routes". (b) NGS is a SPARSE OVERLAY not a population feature — 39.5% of targeted WR/TE player-weeks in 2024, 0% of zero rows ⇒ needs an explicit present/absent flag, ⛔ NEVER impute 0 (imputed-0 separation reads as a bad receiver = "unmeasured⇒bad").
 🩸 **THREE POINT-IN-TIME DEFECTS that would have shipped SILENTLY (CI mocks IO):** (1) `schedules.temp`/`wind` are REALIZED game-book weather, NOT forecasts — 0/177 unplayed 2026 games carry a temp vs 173/178 played 2024; a hard leak BOTH ways; NO NFL weather ingestion exists at all. (2) nflverse DELETED `injuries.date_modified` in 2025 (the ONLY real as-of timestamp in the free stack); our `schema_mode='merge'` backfilled NULLs so it still appears present; `report_status` ~46% non-null and NULL ≠ healthy. (3) `depth_charts` schema REPLACED at 2025 (`week`/`depth_team` 100% NULL; ~554k daily ESPN snapshots on a new `dt` stamp vs ~37k weekly rows). ⭐ 2025 produced THREE separate upstream schema changes in one season → RE-VERIFY schemas before ANY schema-dependent NFL build.
 🎯 **THE CERTIFIED FRAME + the finding that constrains NF-W1's metric:** `stats_player_week` looks ideal but SILENTLY DELETES THE ZERO ATOM (2024 wk1: 983 rostered skill players, only 346 with a stats row) → fitting on it trains on the conditional-on-playing population while serving a user whose whole risk is the guy not playing. CERTIFIED FRAME = **89,954 player-weeks, 2016–2025 REG, 46.1% retained zeros**, roster-FIRST + stats left-joined + byes constructed, 3 zero classes kept distinct (bye / inactive / dressed-no-stat). ⭐⭐ **MAE IS INVERTED AT QB AND TE (conditional median exactly 0.0 in all 10 seasons), NOT at RB (~1.5) / WR (~2.0)** — per NF-D14 the median-at-the-floor is the actual test, not the zero share ⇒ a single pooled metric is wrong for HALF the board → **NF-W1 MUST select on CRPS + score an all-zero DEGENERATE CEILING every run (measure it, don't reason).**
 ⏭️ **UNMEASURED — ⛔ do NOT treat as settled:** `fct_player_week` vs this frame (different populations ~590 depth-chart-driven vs ~500 game-day-roster/wk; zero-atom retention UNMEASURED → NF-W1 diffs row-counts+zero-share per season before substituting); nflverse release lag esp. MNF (a Tue-AM build may miss Monday night — no capture history to measure); `snap_counts` unmatched rate through the `pfr_id` bridge; PFR licensing (fine for fitting, re-read before PFR-derived values reach a PAYING surface). (Original prompt ↓.)

▶ NF-W0a — V0-INFRA: POINT-IN-TIME IMMUTABLE CAPTURE STORE + FAIL-CLOSED LEAKAGE GUARDS (⭐ incl. the TIME-CRITICAL forward capture)   [Model/data-infra · 🧭 OPUS · V0-tier · NO app surface (safe while app sessions are live) · from the v3 architecture §13 + NF-W0's findings 2026-08-05]   🔴 **READY + TIME-CRITICAL — the weather + market forward-capture legs MUST start BEFORE the 2026-09-10 opener or the season's training data is PERMANENTLY LOST**
✅ **DONE 2026-08-05 (branch `nf-w0a-pit-capture`, PR → `dev`) — BOTH gate halves delivered; all four capture legs LIVE-VERIFIED end-to-end. `best_alpha` N/A, $0 new spend. Report: `ablation_results/nf_w0a_pit_capture_store.md`.**
 🚨 **ONE DATED OPERATOR ACTION: the PAID market schedule (`sports_nfl_pit_market_schedule`) ships `default_status=STOPPED` and MUST be turned ON before the opener (2026-09-09 — SEA-NE; the 09-10 date in the original prompt is the Melbourne game) or NO Tue/Fri market feature is EVER backtestable (only closing lines exist historically). The two FREE legs (weather hourly ladder / metadata Tue+Fri) ship `RUNNING` deliberately — a STOPPED schedule silently never runs (E11.23) and would be discovered in January with the season already unrecoverable.**
 ⭐ **TWO NEW POINT-IN-TIME DEFECTS, neither in NF-W0, both found by probing the live release: (1) `roof` IS A POST-HOC FIELD AT RETRACTABLE VENUES — the `temp`/`wind` class one column over. ALL 43 blank-`roof` rows in the entire nflverse release are unplayed 2026 games at exactly the 5 retractable venues (ARI/DAL/HOU/ATL/IND); those venues carry `dome` ZERO times and historically carry `closed` (227) / `open` (32) — values that exist only AFTER the game-day roof decision. ⇒ a blank roof = UNKNOWN-AT-PROJECTION-TIME, so capture FAILS OPEN (skipping blanks would permanently lose every ARI/DAL/HOU/ATL/IND home game's weather) with `roof_known=false` stored; and the historical `closed`/`open` is NOT PIT-safe — using it in a Tuesday build is a LEAK and gating training on it while gating serving on a blank produces train/serve skew. (2) THE HOME TEAM'S STADIUM IS THE WRONG PLACE FOR A NEUTRAL-SITE GAME — 2026 schedules EIGHT international games listed under the home TEAM (`2026_11_MIN_SF` is home_team SF but played at Estadio Banorte in MEXICO CITY; `2026_01_SF_LA` is home_team LA at the MELBOURNE Cricket Ground). The home coords return entirely plausible numbers for the wrong hemisphere with no error anywhere, and `stadium_id` does NOT rescue it (Melbourne carries `LAX01`, the TEAM's code) ⇒ neutral sites resolve by stadium NAME and an unrecognised neutral venue is REFUSED, never fetched at the home stadium. All 272 games of 2026 resolve: 8 neutral, 52 fixed-dome skipped, 0 refused.**
 🩹 **A THIRD DEFECT, found in this story's OWN first cut — A REVISION DETECTOR WITH A 100% FALSE-POSITIVE RATE IS WORSE THAN NONE:** Open-Meteo stamps `generationtime_ms` on every response, so two byte-identical forecasts hashed differently and EVERY benign re-fire alerted as a "vendor revision" (the over-paging monitor that gets muted → the one real revision goes unread). Two-part cure: a DECLARED volatile-key exclusion from the content hash **with the excluded set STORED on the row** (`hash_excluded_keys` — a hash that silently ignores fields is its own silent-death risk), plus PER-SOURCE revision semantics: `REVISION_SEMANTICS` (injuries/schemas — a stable record, so a changed payload IS the §13 revised-record case → keep original, ALERT) vs `LIVE_VALUE_SEMANTICS` (weather/market — the feed MOVES by design, so a re-read inside one checkpoint is expected → keep first, do NOT alert). In both cases the original stands; `semantics` changes only how the difference is REPORTED.
 🏗️ **THE STORE IS APPEND-ONLY, and that IS the design: `ingest/s3io.write_season_partition` writes `mode="overwrite"` + `replaceWhere season=N` — correct for a REPRODUCIBLE feed, FATAL for a PIT capture, which is a MEASUREMENT OF A MOMENT** (overwriting Tuesday's forecast with Friday's destroys the only copy of what was knowable on Tuesday; no archive returns it). `pit/store.py` never issues an overwrite (source-inspection guard that STRIPS COMMENTS first so prose can't satisfy it — INC-38; RED-proven by reintroducing `mode="overwrite"`), partitions by `capture_date`, retains raw payloads write-once (HEAD-before-PUT), and dedups via a DETERMINISTIC `capture_id` over (source, subject, checkpoint) so a re-fired cron is a no-op instead of a duplicate.
 ⏰ **THE LADDER ADAPTATION IS LOAD-BEARING: MLB's `ingest_weather.py` tops out at T-24h (a baseball build is same-day), but an NFL TUESDAY build for a Sunday game stands ~120h out ⇒ an MLB-verbatim ladder gives the Tue/Fri builds NO WEATHER AT ALL** — the exact feature the capture exists to make backtestable. Ladder `[120,72,48,24,3,1]`h, pinned by tests (max≥120; no two rungs can match one moment; an hourly cron cannot miss one).
 📸 **THE SCHEMA LEG INDEPENDENTLY REPRODUCES BOTH OF NF-W0's 2025 BREAKS, from the RELEASE FILE** (`depth_charts` missing `week`/`depth_team`/`position` = the schema REPLACEMENT; `injuries` missing `date_modified` = the DELETED as-of stamp; `schedules` healthy) — **our own lake structurally CANNOT see this** (`schema_mode='merge'` backfills a dropped column with NULLs so it still reads as present), while the vendor's release file has no such amnesia. It also probes null RATES for the small high-value assets, because "present but 100% NULL" is the second silent-death signature a pure schema check misses.
 🧪 **LEAKAGE GUARD — 14 cases DELIBERATELY CONSTRUCTED + a MECHANICAL NON-VACUITY PROOF (NF-D17): `TestTheGuardIsNotVacuous` DISABLES the owning clause and requires each case to become CLEAN, so a fixture that trips two clauses proves neither. ⭐ IT EARNED ITS KEEP IMMEDIATELY — `check_market_phase` originally compared projection-vs-CAPTURE, which fires only when the timestamp clause fires anyway (dead weight); the test caught it, and the fix compares projection-vs-KICKOFF so it now catches MISLABELLED/MIS-STAMPED boards while still permitting the Sunday-morning re-projection to consume a near-closing board (a blanket ban would break the product, not protect it). SOURCE-DELETION RED PROOF: deleting each clause turns 5/11/4/5/6 tests red.** All five `*_timestamp > projection` comparisons are checked (not just §13's three — `ingestion` and `vendor_release` are equally disqualifying); anything UNEVALUABLE is a REJECTION (NF1.7 (a)).
 📏 LIVE-VERIFIED 2026-08-05: weather 3 real forecasts stored (incl. **Wembley 60.5°F — London, not Jacksonville**) · market **272 events × 10 books** (the board is ALREADY fully priced 35 days pre-opener ⇒ enabling early also captures pre-season line movement at no extra cost) · injuries **6,068 rows** with our own stamp · schema **30 assets**. Fast gate 5,736✅ / slow 26✅ (134 new tests); NO dbt files touched.
 ⏭️ **NOT DONE (honest scope, for NF-W1): (a) NO dbt staging over `nfl/pit/*` — nothing reads the store yet (correct: NF-W1 is the first consumer, but the tables are unexercised by any downstream contract); (b) THE GUARD IS A LIBRARY, NOT YET WIRED INTO A BUILD — ⭐ NF-W1 MUST call `assert_point_in_time` at its feature-assembly boundary, else this ships as the "wired ≠ invoked" defect (NF-C0e); (c) NO backfill mode, because there is nothing to backfill — a missed checkpoint is an absent `capture_id` for that rung, permanently; (d) player PROPS are OFF by default (`NFL_PIT_CAPTURE_PROPS`) at ~120cr/event ≈ 75,000cr/season.**
🌿 **FIRST ACTION — WORKTREE off `dev` (app sessions live): `git worktree add ../nf-w0a-pit-capture -b nf-w0a-pit-capture dev`** (⛔ never `dev`/`main`, ⛔ never `git add -A`; `git fetch && git checkout -b … origin/dev` if the operator pre-made it; fresh worktree has NO `.env` → `cp` it; CLAUDE.md 🌿).
⏰ **TIME-CRITICAL — WHY THIS IS TOP OF THE NON-APP QUEUE (NF-W0's #6):** a forecast is only PIT-honest if CAPTURED AT THE TIME — the Open-Meteo archive returns OBSERVATIONS, not the forecast current on a historical Tuesday. Opener is **2026-09-10**; every uncaptured week is permanently absent from the training frame. Same logic for **Tue/Fri MARKET snapshots** — only CLOSING lines exist historically, so no Tuesday-build market feature can EVER be backtested without forward capture. ⇒ START the weather + market capture FIRST, even ahead of the full immutable-store build.
 🌦️ **WEATHER (cheap — reuse MLB's `scripts/ingest_weather.py`, both legs already exist):** `forecast_pregame` (weekly build) + `forecast_intraday` (hours-to-kickoff). Open-Meteo is free/no-key; stadium lat/long already in `stg_nfl_team_geo` (32 teams). THREE adaptations: (1) extend the checkpoint ladder to ~`[120, 72, 48, 24, 3, 1]` hours — ⚠️ MLB's max is T-24h, but an NFL TUESDAY build is ~5 days out, so copying MLB verbatim gives the Tue/Fri builds NO weather at all; (2) write S3-native to `nfl/raw/weather/`; (3) gate on the PER-GAME `roof`, NOT the team's informational `is_dome_home`.
 📈 **MARKET (Tue/Fri snapshots):** capture the intraday NFL lines/props on a Tue/Fri cadence so an early-build market feature is backtestable — closing-only history can't support it.
 🩹 **INJURIES (own timestamp) — NF-W0's defect #2:** nflverse deleted `injuries.date_modified`, so CAPTURE injuries WITH OUR OWN `capture_timestamp` going forward (the only as-of timestamp left is one we make).
 📸 **SCHEMA SNAPSHOTS:** snapshot the nflverse schemas PER INGEST so a silent field deletion (the 3 that hit in 2025) can't recur undetected.
🎯 WHY (the store itself): the weekly system re-projects multiple times per week (Mon prior → Wed/Fri practice → Sun inactives/lines) and its whole credibility rests on NEVER leaking future information into an earlier build. This is the data backbone V1 sits on.
🔬 DO (doc §13): the immutable snapshot store with the timestamp-key contract (`projection_timestamp`, `feature_timestamp`, `source_timestamp`, `capture_timestamp`, `vendor_release_timestamp`, `ingestion_timestamp`); FAIL-CLOSED enforcement (reject a build when any `*_timestamp > projection_timestamp`, provenance missing, a revised vendor record replaced the captured original, a closing line/late prop joins an earlier projection, or a rolling window contains the target/future game); raw payloads retained immutably; ⭐ leakage UNIT/INTEGRATION tests that DELIBERATELY construct each leakage case and REQUIRE rejection (RED-prove them). DuckDB/S3-native (SF-free).
🚦 **SEQUENCING — SHIP THE CAPTURE FIRST, BUILD THE FRAMEWORK AROUND IT:** the calendar clock (2026-09-10) is on the FORWARD-CAPTURE legs, NOT the immutable-store framework. ⇒ land a MINIMAL live weather + Tue/Fri-market + own-timestamp-injury capture (writing raw immutably to S3 with the timestamp-key stamps) AS THE FIRST deliverable — even before the full fail-closed leakage-guard framework — so no week is lost; then build out the §13 store + leakage guards around the data already accruing. ⛔ do NOT let "build the whole immutable store + RED-prove every leakage case" delay the first live capture past the opener.
🔒 `best_alpha` N/A. Reuse the repo's existing snapshot/capture patterns (MLB `ingest_weather.py` both legs, odds capture) where they fit. Branch + PR → `dev`.
GATE/AC: **(1 — TIME-CRITICAL, ships FIRST) live forward capture RUNNING before the 2026-09-10 opener:** weather (`forecast_pregame` + `forecast_intraday` on the ~[120,72,48,24,3,1]h ladder, per-game `roof`, S3-native `nfl/raw/weather/`), Tue/Fri market snapshots, injuries stamped with OUR `capture_timestamp`, and a per-ingest nflverse schema snapshot — each writing raw immutably with the timestamp-key stamps; **(2) the immutable PIT capture store + fail-closed leakage guard** with the full timestamp-key contract, RED-proven leakage tests covering every §13 rejection case, built around the accruing data; SF-free; branch + PR. ⚠️ RUNTIME-GATE: the capture crons must be VERIFIED firing live (a captured row with a real `capture_timestamp` before first pitch), not just coded — CI mocks IO.

▶ NF1 — WEEKLY + SEASON FANTASY-POINT PROJECTIONS (the core fantasy model)   [Model · 🧭 OPUS · §0.5 · consumes the N1.2 shared player-week core]   (was N1.3; operator-scoped 2026-07)
✅ **CODE-COMPLETE + BAKE-OFF RUN 2026-07-26 (final `build --s3` + full 6-season NF-D3 grade = operator's remaining laptop runs).** Built the JOINT LEARNED re-weighting (`nf1_model.py` pure + `run_nf1.py` CLI/IO) + the matchup-aware weekly leg, per the operator's two 2026-07-26 scope calls: **(a) FULL matchup-aware weekly** (season per-game × defense-vs-position × Vegas implied-team-points × home, Gamma posterior-predictive) and **(b) MARKET-BLIND** (NO ADP/ECR — orthogonal NF-D signals only; ADP/ECR stay the NF-D3 benchmark). §0.5 bake-off (4 candidates, Optuna, walk-forward CV 2019–2025, oracle-checked): **WINNER = GBM** (pooled within-pos ρ **0.732 vs MVP-1 null 0.718**). ⭐ **The discriminator is QB (the flagged-weakest position): the two LINEAR blends REGRESS QB below the null (ridge 0.632 / enet 0.619 vs 0.648); GBM is the ONLY candidate that IMPROVES QB (0.661) while topping pooled ρ.** GBM overfit-gap 0.071 (small-data caution; selection on held-out). Feature ablation: all groups contribute, **`age` biggest (−0.010)** = the one feature NF1 adds over MVP-1. 🐛 **FIXED a level-vs-ordering design bug (the E2.1-r discrimination≠pricing lesson):** the learner trains on ≥6g realized fp = a SURVIVORSHIP-inflated LEVEL >> MVP-1's risk-adjusted level → a direct raw-line rescale saturated the clamp + scrambled the validated ordering (a rookie QB floated to #1). CURE = `apply_learned_ordering` — a within-position quantile REMAP (the `blend_adp_prior` mechanism): reorder by the learned score, keep MVP-1's calibrated point MULTISET → ships the EXACT bake-off ordering on calibrated levels (nf1_scale 1.5-saturated → mean 1.03; board face-valid: Lamar/Herbert/Burrow/Hurts/Allen top; season interval CALIBRATED κ=1.725→calib_80 0.805 floor met). **Weekly = §0.5 NULL (60 wks 2021–2024): the scalar matchup tilts do NOT lift weekly ordering (Δ vs flat ≤0: +dvp −0.003/+env −0.007/+home +0.001/full −0.013) → DROPPED (NGS slice-2 precedent); ship the FLAT calibrated season-per-game-per-week baseline, DVP/implied-pts carried as UI matchup CONTEXT only.** Weekly interval a FLAGGED Gamma approx (PIT ~0.23, zero-inflated → recalibrate before weekly pricing; future lever = a stat-level opponent model, not a scalar tilt). ⚖️ **NF-D3 grade (full 6-season 2019–2024) = INCUMBENT-STANDS-for-the-product (honest, no overclaim): the bake-off metric (full-universe ρ) and the product metric DISAGREE.** NF1 beats MVP-1 on full-universe ρ (0.732 vs 0.718) but on the consensus-COVERED (draftable) tier it's MIXED: BETTER vs ADP (Δρ −0.031 vs MVP-1 −0.060; TE +0.092; **ADP-fade edge IMPROVED to 0.540 vs MVP-1 0.478**) yet WORSE than MVP-1 vs the deep systems (ECR −0.074/ESPN −0.079/Sleeper −0.157) + worse at top-tier QB. WHY: GBM's regression-to-mean lifts DEPTH ordering (the full-universe metric) but COMPRESSES the studs (the tier that matters); and market-blind ⇒ the top-tier QB/RB gap is STRUCTURAL (that gap IS the excluded market signal). ⇒ **SHIP: MVP-1 STANDS as the served draft board; NF1 ships as a CALIBRATED ALTERNATIVE (own S3 prefix) + the research baseline. Airtight claim UNCHANGED + slightly stronger on one axis: "our high-conviction ADP fades out-predict the market 0.54 vs 0.31" — never "we beat consensus."** The real top-tier lever = a MARKET-AWARE variant (revisit the market-blind scope call — ADP/ECR as position-conditional features); optional §0.5 follow-up = re-select the learner on a TOP-TIER-weighted metric (may favor ridge/null). Report: `ablation_results/nf1_model_report.md`. Fast gate 2042✅ (20 NF1 tests). ⏭️ OPERATOR: `git add` the NF1 files; NF3-lite/MVP-2 keep MVP-1 (do NOT auto-repoint to NF1 — it doesn't beat MVP-1 on the product metric).
🟢🟢 **READY-TO-RUN (cleared 2026-07-22) — but see NF-FASTPATH for the NEAR-TERM 8/22 deliverable (that ships first; NF1 is the full refine).**
🧱 **NF1 = MVP-1 + the NF-D DATA FOUNDATION (2026-07-24): the full refine's lift over MVP-1 comes from the NF-D2 usage/context sources (snap share, NGS/PFR, vacated opportunity, Vegas environment, ADP) ABLATED in — that's what closes the within-tier ordering gap vs The Fantasy Footballers MVP-1 exposed. Grade against the NF-D3 benchmark scorecard. Sequence NF-D2 (+NF-D1's 2026 data) before/with NF1.**

CURE = `safe_spearman` (distinct-value + finite guards) + score a degenerate prediction ρ=0.0 on scoreable seasons (NEVER skip, or a config grades on a friendlier season subset than the null). **Applies to ANY small-data per-group bake-off — NCAAF/MiLB harnesses too.** ⭐ **STRATEGIC:** (1) the QB / deep-system gap IS the market signal — no market-blind feature work has touched it across NF1/NF1.1/NF-D2/D4/D7; (2) the EVIDENCED next lever = the MARKET-AWARE variant (NF1.3) — an IDENTITY decision, not a modeling gap; (3) the narrow public claim strengthened again ("where we most disagree with ADP we out-predict it," fade 0.543 vs 0.274 across all 3 generations); (4) MVP-1 is now a PROVEN incumbent (3 challengers failed honest gates — the E2.1-r tied-field reading paying off). NO serving/deploy downstream — MVP-1 board + NF-C1/NF-C2 untouched, nothing redeploys. **NF1.2 + NF1.3 REUSE `nf1_1_model.py`'s per-position machinery + `top_tier_rho` + the deflation kit as-is; keep the xFP features in the candidate set for QB/RB/TE ONLY (learned signal there, NULL at WR).** Near-misses (WR DSR 0.865 vs 0.95; RB) are cheaply re-testable after the 2026 season adds a walk-forward fold. 🛠️ **Harness (shipped 2026-07-27).** Shipped: `nf1_1_model.py` (NEW, pure) — per-position learners `pos_ridge`/`pos_gbm`/**`pos_similarity` (the comparables/analog learner: k-nearest historical player-seasons in the z-scaled position feature space, tunable `mvp1_emphasis` on the incumbent axis, inverse-distance-power comp average)** vs the `pos_null` MVP-1 foil; PRE-REGISTERED position feature sets (QB drops `target_share`, keeps `carry_share` + `xrush_td_pg`/`td_luck_ratio`; RB gets all 6 xFP; WR/TE drop `carry_share`, receiving xFP legs) = the NF-D7 features consumed as LEARNED candidates only (heuristic blend not re-litigated); **selection metric = `top_tier_rho`** (top-N/pos = QB24/RB36/WR48/TE24, tier ANCHORED ON THE MVP-1 INCUMBENT so every candidate grades on the SAME subset — can't game its own tier; oracle-ceiling-checked) + the full deflation harness (**`cscv_pbo` over season-columns + `config_spread` (the tied-field-vs-wide discriminator) + `deflated_sharpe` (winner lift vs null, deflated by EVERY trial incl. ablation arms) + `bh_fdr` across the 4 position searches**; repoint gate = beats-null AND PBO<0.2 AND DSR≥0.95 AND FDR-surviving, else MVP-1 stands per position). `run_nf1_1.py` (NEW, CLI): `bakeoff` (Optuna 40/class/pos, every trial counted) / `grade` (NF-D3 scorecard on the NF1.1 board, prints stored MVP-1+NF1 aggregates for apples-to-apples) / `build` (per-position winners → **`apply_learned_ordering` RANK-ONLY** over MVP-1's calibrated multiset, κ-calibrated 80% interval, OWN S3 prefix `nf1_1_season_projections`); pool = the cached NF1 feature pool + per-base-season xFP join (assemble-once). `betting_ml/tests/test_nf1_1_model.py` (NEW, 28 fast-gate tests). Fast gate 2097✅; smoke bakeoff+grade verified on the real lake. ⚠️ **Degeneracy fix (first full-run attempt NaN'd the QB pos_gbm search):** a `min_child_samples`≳33 GBM on ~33-68 QB train rows predicts a near-constant carrying ~1e-14 float noise → `std==0` guard missed it → scipy `spearmanr` NaN → Optuna "value nan" + a NaN-poisoned best-record sort. CURE = `safe_spearman` (pairwise-dropna + ≥2-DISTINCT-values guard + finite check) and — the selection-honesty half — **a degenerate (constant) prediction over a scoreable tier scores ρ=0.0 instead of skipping the season** (a constant projection has zero ordering skill; a skip would grade the config on a friendlier season subset than the null). Verified: the exact failing hp now scores mean_top 0.258 with full null-identical season coverage. ⏭️ operator: full `bakeoff` → `grade` → verdict (+`build --s3` only if a survivor repoints). Two fixable causes: (a) selected on the WRONG metric (full-universe ρ ≠ the draft-tier goal = the E2.1-r selection-metric lesson), (b) POOLING all positions into one GBM regresses the STUDS to the mean = exactly the tier the board cares about. NF1 v1 was only 2 passes + never modeled positions INDEPENDENTLY (the linear candidates were per-position, but the WINNER was pooled). This is the honest market-blind next pass.

▶ NF1.3 — MARKET-AWARE NF1 VARIANT (position-conditional ADP/ECR — the top-tier QB/RB lever)   [Model + product · 🧭 OPUS · 🔬 §0.5]   (NEW 2026-07-27, operator — decision resolved)
✅✅ **DONE 2026-07-27 — PRODUCT WIN at QB/RB; the BEST generation ever on the product metric → a DUAL-BOARD outcome.** Built `nf1_3_model.py`/`run_nf1_3.py` reusing the NF1.1 harness VERBATIM (`top_tier_rho` + CSCV-PBO/DSR/BH-FDR + `safe_spearman` + the 3 blind learners) + a unified `market_rank` feature (ECR-primary, ADP-fallback — ECR covers 2025 FFC-ADP lacks), `PosMarketBlend` (per-position blend weight w∈[0,1]; w→0 blind, w→1 pure consensus), + `PosMarketOnly` (pure-consensus foil → "does blending beat just SHOWING the market?"). Leakage-safe per-season ADP/ECR join; honest per-position `market_lean` provenance served; own S3 prefix (never overwrites MVP-1/NF1/NF1.1). 19 fast-gate tests (suite 2155). **RESULTS (base 2017–24, 40 trials/class, 7 seasons):** FIRST board to BEAT ADP overall (**+0.015 pooled** vs MVP-1 −0.060 / NF1 −0.031 / NF1.1 −0.020); best-ever vs ECR (−0.020). **QB vs ADP −0.15 → +0.032 (a +0.18 swing); RB −0.101 → +0.006;** QB vs ECR −0.109 → −0.015. Per-position lean AS HYPOTHESIZED (QB/RB market-led w≥0.66; WR market-informed GBM; TE moderate blend); every position crushed the blind MVP-1 null on top-tier ρ (+0.09…+0.19). ⚠️ **HONEST CORE / TRADE-OFF:** incorporating ADP dropped the ADP-fade edge **0.543 → 0.478** (fewer/milder disagreements — BY DESIGN), and the top-tier-ρ lift is **NOT deflation-robust (PBO 0.23–0.69; only WR clears DSR)** → this is "TRACKS THE MARKET + adds our signal," NOT an independent edge. The deep independent systems (Sleeper −0.163, ESPN −0.100) remain the ceiling. The FANTASY gate = product-metric + calibration (NOT the betting PBO/DSR — roadmap §0) → NF1.3 CLEARS its gate = a shippable product win at the two positions users care most about on draft day. ⇒ **DUAL-BOARD (the honest resolution): serve the market-aware board as the QB/RB user product (per-position `market_lean` labels, framed "we INCORPORATE market consensus" — ⛔ NEVER "we beat the market"), KEEP market-blind MVP-1 as the fade-claim baseline.** These answer two different questions — do NOT collapse them. ⛔ **SERVING IS AN OPEN OPERATOR DECISION — no board landed** (`--mode build --board beats-null --s3` produces it; the `gated` board is EMPTY — nothing passed strict deflation → serve beats-null or nothing). REUSABLE: `build_market_features` / `PosMarketBlend` / `PosMarketOnly` / `MARKET_FEATURES` now in the shared harness → NF1.5 sweeps the market axes + has a market-aware QB/RB incumbent to beat. **REFINEMENTS (for NF1.5 / a v2):** (1) blend the market against the LEARNED model, not just MVP-1; (2) ⭐ UNCERTAINTY-WEIGHTED market term via `market_dispersion` (expert rank_std) — lean on consensus only where experts AGREE → preserves fades where consensus is SOFT; (3) add the 2026 fold (cheap PBO re-test — RB PBO 0.229 may cross); (4) watch ESPN regression (thin 2-season); (5) verify interval calibration on the full build (smoke κ≈1.8, calib_80 0.805, PIT flatness 0.214 mild). Files: `nf1_3_model.py`, `run_nf1_3.py`, 19 tests. 🔒 **SERVING DECISION (operator 2026-07-27): HELD — the market-BLIND MVP-1 board stays the SINGLE served board; NF1.3's market-aware board is BUILT-NOT-SERVED; serving is REVISITED at NF1.5 (which has a market-aware incumbent + the refinements above).** ⛔ no user-facing rankings/copy references a market-aware or QB/RB-consensus board until it actually ships. ⏭️ operator: `git add`. (Orig prompt ↓.)

The per-position LEAN came out exactly as hypothesized: **QB/RB market-LED** (`pos_market_blend`, w≥0.66), **WR market-INFORMED** (`pos_gbm` — barely edges pure consensus 0.607 v 0.595, keeps its own signal), **TE market-BLEND** (moderate w). ▶ NF1.4 — ROOKIE-MODEL REFINEMENT (fix the flagged rookie OVER-VALUATION)   [Model · 🧭 OPUS · 🔬 §0.5]   (NEW 2026-07-27, deep-dive + NF1's deferred defect)
✅✅ **DONE 2026-07-27 — CLEAN NULL on the model search + a REAL correctness fix; ⭐ THE STORY'S PREMISE WAS REFUTED (rookie over-valuation is NOT a level error).** Walk-forward over draft classes 2019–25: the incumbent rookie prior is COLD at every position (`tier_bias` QB −32 / RB −58 / WR −49 / TE −44 PPR), and its point projection for the flagged #1-overall rookie QB is nearly UNBIASED (+8.9 vs ~201 realized) → recalibrating the level DOWN would make the board WORSE. ⭐ **THE DOGFOODING SYMPTOM IS A RANK EFFECT FROM VARIANCE, not a level bug:** the #1-overall QB projects QB11–15, finishes ~QB19.5 (~6 slots high); rookie QB outcomes are hugely dispersed (Murray +85, Lawrence −62) + veteran QBs pack densely there, so a few points buy many ranks → an UNCERTAINTY problem. SURVIVORSHIP RED HERRING (looked like the answer): `load_rookie_training` fits under `where games>0`, dropping 15% of drafted skill rookies / 35% of QBs (inflates the survivor QB mean 67→104) — tested as its own arm (incumbent form, full population): held-out accuracy got WORSE everywhere (QB 74.8→77.1) → the board was already too COLD; a real estimation flaw but NOT the flagged-board cause (matters for the INTERVAL though). BAKE-OFF NULL: 134 pre-registered configs × 4 pos (6 learner forms × 5 feature blocks × shrink λ), walk-forward; NO position repoints (QB 0 do-no-harm candidates; RB 80.4→71.8 MAE but PBO 0.343; WR p=0.48 / TE p=0.23 fail BH-FDR) = the **FOURTH deflated fantasy null** (NF1/NF1.1/NF1.2/NF1.4). ⭐ **WHAT SHIPPED (a CORRECTNESS fix, NOT a search result — a mis-stated interval is a DEFECT, no deflation gate, like a null-handling bug): the ROOKIE 80% INTERVAL** — MVP-1's multiplicative `fp×cv` band covered only 0.680 (QB 0.444) + collapsed toward zero where late rookies surprise → REPLACED with an empirical per-position TERCILE band off what drafted rookies actually scored (full drafted population, so a late pick's p10 can honestly be 0). Coverage **0.680→0.790** (QB 0.444→0.741, RB→0.831, WR→0.812, TE→0.720), `point_projection_max_abs_change=0.0` (pinned) — the flagged rookie's "80% range" 5.6–531 → 26.5–277. + `season_projection.rookie_board_face_validity()` advisory gate. ⭐ **IMPLICATIONS:** (1) **RETIRE "rookie over-valuation" as a stated defect** (it was carried in NF1's deferred list + the MVP-3 dogfooding notes as a LEVEL problem — it ISN'T): the point projection is the incumbent slot curve, UNCHANGED + nearly unbiased; if it resurfaces, the honest framing is "rookie RANK UNCERTAINTY at the top of the QB class," fix = variance not level. (2) NF1.5/NF2 treat the rookie point projection as the INCUMBENT to beat, NOT a broken component. (3) NF2 (dynasty) inherits a DIFFERENT target — NF1.4 priced the ROOKIE SEASON (redraft); multi-year rookie value is a separate model. **OPEN:** (a) `fantasy_engine/scoring.py` re-symmetrises the band (league rescore derives its interval from one `_sd` → width propagates but the asymmetric SHAPE doesn't) → an NF1.5 / NF-C1 slice; (b) rookie ADP/ECR is thin → NF1.3's market lever is weak for rookies (relevant if NF1.5 sweeps market axes); (c) tercile band is coarse at the top (more buckets need more data — deliberately NOT tuned to avoid an un-deflated search on the band; the measured coverage is the honest claim). Harness `nf1_4_rookie.py` + cached `nf1_4_rookie_training.parquet` (872 drafted rookies 2016–26). Fast gate 2261 ✅ (3× under -n auto). ⏭️ operator: `git add`. (Orig prompt ↓.)
✅✅ **DONE 2026-07-27 — MODEL VERDICT: CLEAN NULL (the slot curve STANDS), and the story's HOT-CURVE PREMISE IS REFUTED as a level claim. ONE fix ships: the rookie 80% interval.** Built `nf1_4_rookie.py` (pure) + `run_nf1_4.py` (IO: `assemble`/`bakeoff`/`board`) reusing NF1.1's deflation kit + `safe_spearman` VERBATIM. Rookie training frame assembled ONCE → `artifacts/nf1_4_rookie_training.parquet` (872 drafted skill rookies, classes 2016–2026; P1A `projected_nfl_z` backbone + combine measurables from `xref_college_nfl_players` + recruiting pedigree + a DERIVED breakout-age block; every feature pre-debut, SF-free, sports-lake DuckDB). §0.5 bake-off = **134 pre-registered configs × 4 positions**, walk-forward by DRAFT CLASS 2019–2025, 6 learner forms (incumbent-through-the-real-code-path / pos_mean / pos_median / slot_eb / slot_ridge / slot_gbm) × 5 pre-registered feature blocks (slot always-on · p1a · athletic · breakout · recruit) × shrink λ∈{0,.15,.30,.50}. **RESULT: NO position repoints.** QB — ZERO eligible candidates (none stayed within the do-no-harm rho of the incumbent's 0.635); RB beat it on tier MAE (80.4→71.8) but PBO 0.343 ≥ 0.2; WR (68.84→68.67, p=0.48) and TE (53.9→52.5, p=0.23) fail BH-FDR. ⚠️⚠️ **THE PREMISE CORRECTION (the most important finding): the incumbent rookie prior is COLD, not hot — `tier_bias` is NEGATIVE at every position (QB −32 / RB −58 / WR −49 / TE −44 PPR) and its point projection for the #1-overall rookie QB is nearly UNBIASED over 7 classes (+8.9 PPR vs a realized mean of ~201).** The `where games > 0` SURVIVORSHIP filter in `load_rookie_training` IS real and large (15% of drafted skill rookies never play — 35% of QBs — inflating the survivor-only QB mean 67→104, +54%), but an arm re-fitting the incumbent's EXACT form (zero new DOF) on the full drafted population made held-out accuracy WORSE at every position (QB 74.8→77.1, RB 80.4→88.2) — the board was already too cold, so lowering it further loses. **So what IS the dogfooding symptom? A RANK effect, not a level one:** on the 2019–25 boards the #1-overall QB is projected QB11–15 and finishes QB8–QB25 (mean ≈QB19.5) — ~6 slots high — because rookie QB outcomes are enormously dispersed (Murray beat his projection by 85 PPR, Lawrence missed by 62) and veteran QB projections pack densely at that level, so a few points buy many ranks. **A rank error from variance around an unbiased point estimate is an UNCERTAINTY problem, not a level one** ⇒ ⭐ **THE ONE SHIPPED FIX: the rookie 80% band.** MVP-1's `fp × cv` parameter band (cv estimated on the SAME survivor-filtered sample; its own report said "recalibrate before pricing") covers **0.680 of its nominal 0.80 — and 0.444 at QB**; it is a decoration, and being multiplicative it collapses toward zero exactly where late-round rookies most surprise. Replaced with an EMPIRICAL band (`fit_rookie_slot_curves(hist, band_hist=...)` → per-position q10/q90 of what drafted rookies in each prediction TERCILE actually scored, over the FULL drafted population so a late pick's p10 can honestly be 0): coverage **0.680→0.790** overall, **QB 0.444→0.741**, RB 0.757→0.831, WR 0.750→0.812, TE 0.600→0.720 — with `point_projection_max_abs_change = 0.0` (byte-identical; pinned by `test_calibrated_band_does_NOT_move_the_point_projection`). `uncertainty_type` now reports `calibrated` vs the legacy `parameter`. Also NEW: `season_projection.rookie_board_face_validity()` — the AC's face-validity gate (no rookie at #1/in the overall top-10; no rookie above the Q90 of realized rookie seasons at his position over the FULL drafted population), ADVISORY-tier, wired into the MVP-1 report + logged loudly. ⚠️ THE GATE WAS MIS-SPECIFIED ON ITS FIRST CUT AND IS NOW CORRECTED — worth reading, because it is a reusable lesson about reference classes: the level check first compared the top projected rookie against the Q90 of ALL drafted rookies, which the **REALIZED** top rookie clears in **25 of 28** cohort-positions, so it fired on 7/7 cohorts and on the live 2026 class (a FALSE alarm). The correct reference is the Q`90` of the per-class BEST realized rookie at that position: against it the incumbent breaches **0/28** while reality breaches **9/28**, the 2026 board PASSES, and the gate becomes a real tripwire. ⭐ That silence is itself an independent third confirmation of the story's central finding — the rookie prior runs COLD, never projecting above what a strong class's best rookie actually does. Guards: `test_the_level_cap_references_the_per_class_BEST_rookie_not_the_whole_population` + a thin-reference skip test. Metric hygiene: selection = per-position DRAFTABLE-tier MAE (top QB3/RB6/WR8/TE3, tier ANCHORED on the incumbent so it is identical for every candidate — NF1.1's lesson); the degenerate `pos_median` is IN the field as the MAE-collapse tell; do-no-ordering-harm eligibility; oracle floor holds (though for an MAE-type metric it is a DIRECTION check — the substantive guard is metric monotonicity + the median-in-field, both pinned by tests). 29 fast-gate tests (`test_nf1_4_rookie.py`); fast gate **2259 passed / 7 skipped** ✅. ⚠️ FOLLOW-UP for NF1.5/NF-C1: `fantasy_engine/scoring.py` rescores the league interval from a single `_sd`, which RE-SYMMETRISES the (strongly asymmetric) rookie band — the WIDTH improvement lands but the shape does not; carrying p10/p90 through proportionally is a small league-engine change deliberately left OUT of NF1.4's scope. Reports: `ablation_results/nf1_4_rookie.{md,json}`. ⏭️ operator: re-run the MVP-1 board so the new rookie intervals land (command in the handoff), then `git add`. (Orig prompt ↓.)
🎯 WHY: MVP-3 dogfooding + NF1 both flagged ROOKIE OVER-VALUATION — rookies keep the MVP-1 draft-slot fp-curve, which runs too HOT (a rookie QB floated to #1 overall; Mendoza-type inflation). Research shows the field's rookie levers are DRAFT CAPITAL (dominant) + ATHLETIC PROFILE (combine) + BREAKOUT AGE + college production — blended + SHRUNK, not a hot slot curve.
🔬 DO: REPLACE MVP-1's `project_rookies` draft-SLOT fp-curve (the hot-curve bug — it runs too hot) with a rebuilt rookie prior. ⭐ REUSE P1A's output (`ncaaf_nfl_rookie_projections`) as the BACKBONE — do NOT rebuild the college→NFL translation P1A already did (its verdict: draft slot beats college production, 0.64 vs 0.79 MAE) — + add combine/athletic-profile + breakout-age, all SHRUNK toward a position-mean with WIDE honest uncertainty (a rookie is priors-only). ⚠️ RECALIBRATE THE LEVEL (the hot curve is the bug). §0.5 select vs the current slot-curve on held-out rookie seasons; face-validity (no rookie QB at #1). 🚨🚨 **THIS IS THE HIGHEST MULTIPLE-COMPARISONS RISK STORY IN THE FANTASY PROGRAM — "test many models × many feature combos" IS the data-mining machine §0.5 exists to STOP. Guardrails are NON-NEGOTIABLE:**

Deflation is what makes a BOUNDED search safe; an unbounded one is a mirage even deflated.
 3. **SELECT on the TOP-TIER product metric** (NF1.1 lesson — top-N-per-position ρ + the NF-D3 grade), ANCHORED on the incumbent board so no config games its own tier; oracle-floor checked; `safe_spearman` guard (NF1.1 small-data NaN landmine).
 4. **PER-POSITION independent** (the NF1.1 frame) + KEEP the ordering-not-level `apply_learned_ordering` discipline.
🔒 HONEST FRAME: the INCUMBENT = the CURRENT best served board (MVP-1, or whichever NF1.2/NF1.3/NF1.4 survivor repointed it). NF1.5 must beat THAT on the product metric to repoint; a NULL keeps the incumbent. ⚠️ the market-blind CEILING is already established (NF1 + NF1.1) → a CLEAN NULL is LIKELY + fully valid, and it's VALUABLE: it PROVES the incumbent is the ceiling of the entire accumulated feature library, not merely assumed (the E2.1-r tied-field reading). Include the MLP as ONE deflated candidate to TEST that honestly — do NOT build DL-first, and do NOT exempt it from the deflation gate. Skew/uncertainty DL methods (MC-dropout, Bayesian NN, TFP-SHASH) chase an OUTCOME (calibrated skewed distributions) our partial-pool Bayesian approach ALREADY delivers more sample-efficiently → not a priority here.

✅ **CODE-COMPLETE + RE-GRADE PASSED 2026-08-01; PUBLISH CLEARED (operator to run the publish).** Full record: `ablation_results/nf1_5b_serving_reland.md`. Fast gate green (4235), slow gate green, `tsc --noEmit` clean.
  📊 **RE-GRADE (2019–2024, current code, corrected 784-player universe): ADP Δρ pooled = +0.022 (us 0.517 vs ADP 0.494) against the SERVED MVP-1 board's −0.059** — the flip turns a loss to ADP into a win, on the exact metric the decision was made on. Sleeper is the other apples-to-apples benchmark (both n=6) and is unchanged (−0.118 → −0.125); ECR/ESPN improved but over DIFFERENT season counts, so they are directional only. Interval floor HELD everywhere: pooled `calib_80` 0.847 (n=2688), per position 0.807–0.868, per season 0.832–0.870, **100% `calibrated_per_player`** ⇒ the NF1.9 band survives the re-order.
  ⚠️ **"BEATS ADP AT ALL 4 POSITIONS" DID NOT REPRODUCE — and the story's own WHY line asserts it, so fix it wherever it is quoted.** Current code: QB +0.031, WR +0.037, TE +0.021, **RB −0.000 (a wash)**; per season 4 wins / 1 tie (2023) / **1 loss (2020, −0.044)**. And it is **ADP-SPECIFIC** — ECR (−0.013), ESPN (−0.051) and Sleeper (−0.125) all still order better than we do. ⇒ the sayable claim is **"beats ADP on average over 2019–2024"**, never "at every position", never "every season", never "beats the market". The fade edge is also much weaker: +0.082 margin (0.498 vs 0.416) where MVP-1's was +0.235 (0.478 vs 0.243) — ⚠️ over a DIFFERENT disagreement population, so the honest read is "the fade set changed and the surviving margin is narrow", not a percentage drop.
  🚫 **THE PAYLOAD NOTE CARRIES NO SUPERIORITY CLAIM AT ALL** (deliberate): a bare "beats ADP" on a browse surface has no evidence beside it, contradicts the copy already there ("ADP is a reference point, not a scoreboard"), and needs two qualifications that don't fit in a caption. The claim + its receipts are NF3.2's job; NF1.5b's payload carries only the CAVEAT (which positions lean on the market, and re-ordering-not-re-pricing), which is the part a user cannot look up.
  ⭐ **READINESS LOCK (2) WAS WRONG, AND ITS BEING WRONG WAS THE STORY.** "The refined board is ordering-only ⇒ points AND the NF1.9 intervals are the SAME numbers, so the repoint is a one-line source toggle" describes the DESIGN, not the CODE. NF1.5's build assembled a PARALLEL board (from the NF1.2 research frame) instead of transforming the shipped one, and had drifted on three axes that would ALL have reached users on the flip: **(a) UNIVERSE — 716 players vs the shipped 784**, because the research frame's `load_base_season` has no NF-D11 base-anchor rescue ⇒ **68 returning veterans would have VANISHED from the draft board**; **(b) INTERVAL — every band re-priced as `point ± 1.2816·κ·sd`, the pre-NF1.9 normal whose measured coverage is ~0.55 of nominal 0.80**, stamped `calibrated` ⇒ flipping the board would have silently reverted the NF1.9 per-player band on ~90% of it; **(c) a private rookie leg** drifting from the shipped one. ⇒ **RULE: "ordering-only" is a claim about the ESTIMAND, never evidence that a derived board INHERITS the incumbent's universe, interval or rookie leg — a derived board that RE-DERIVES rather than TRANSFORMS drifts on every axis its author didn't re-check, and the drift is invisible (a plausible-looking band; a missing player looks like a player who isn't projected).** Cure: the refined board is now `build_projection(..., veteran_postprocess=<the re-order>)` — the shipped board with the veteran ORDER re-assigned — and the interval is re-derived through MVP-1's own `season_projection.attach_season_interval` at the re-assigned level (an interval must FOLLOW the point it prices). Rebuilt board: 784 = 784 identical ids, 784/784 `calibrated_per_player`, κ deleted (it was a second private interval model; the verify is now a coverage FLOOR per E2.1-r, not a knob).
  ⭐ **TWO SERVING SURFACES READ TWO SEPARATE ARTIFACTS** — `board_*.json` (Rankings/Optimizer/League Board) comes from `run_league_board.py`, `projections.json` from the exporter. Repointing only the exporter (what lock (1) specified) would have ranked the same player differently on two pages with **no error anywhere**. Both now take `--projection-source`, every board row is stamped `projection_source`, and the export REFUSES on a mismatch — or on a board too old to carry the column, which is exactly the stale pre-flip artifact. **RULE: when a feature has two serving blobs built by two scripts, the consistency must be PROVEN at export time, not sequenced by hand.**
  🔬 Residual, now measured + ALERTed rather than assumed: `apply_learned_level`'s rescale clamp (0.30–3.5) saturates on **20 of 703** veterans, which is why the multiset is preserved to ~0.1–0.3 PPR (max 0.78–4.69) rather than exactly — the shipped mechanism's tail guard, not new.
  ⛔ **THE RE-GRADE IS THE OPEN GATE.** The board is REBUILT and structurally sound (784 players, NF1.9 bands, K/DST folding, superflex still lifts the best QB to overall rank 2), but **beats-ADP has NOT been re-measured on current code** and cannot be inherited (NF1.5's stage-1 pool `n_pool` 6736 predates NF-D11's universe change; NF1.5b moved the universe by 68 players). Operator runs `--mode grade`; if `aggregate.adp.delta_rho_pooled` is still positive → dry-run → publish; if not → **STOP+flag and revert the boards to `--projection-source mvp1`** (the decision was contingent on it reproducing).

▶ NF2 — DYNASTY / ROOKIE BOARD (multi-year hold; the differentiator)   [Model/product · 🧭 OPUS · rookie leg needs P1A ✅]   (was N1.3's Dynasty leg)
🎯 WHY: the Dynasty rookie market is priors-only → an honest model-based board is the underserved differentiator (the GTM Dynasty play). Multi-year hold, not single-season.
DO: rank players/prospects for a multi-season hold. ⭐ **THE ROOKIE LEG CONSUMES NCAAF-P1A AS A RESIDUAL ON THE DRAFT SLOT, NOT standalone:** P1A's verdict = college production is real (PBO 0.000/DSR 0.994) but the DRAFT SLOT beats it (0.64 vs 0.79 MAE) → **rank on draft position + the P1A residual (where `projected_nfl_z` [view `ncaaf_nfl_rookie_projections`, keyed `gsis_id`] disagrees with the draft board), NEVER P1A alone** (worse than the draft board). ⭐ trust at SKILL positions (RB/TE/QB/WR); ≈0 for DL/LB/DB; the `sd` is PARAMETER uncertainty → recalibrate before ranking/pricing off it. Veterans (from NF1) + rookies (P1A residual) combine into the board.
GATE/AC: an honest dynasty board (multi-year, uncertainty-aware); rookie leg = draft-slot + P1A-residual (skill positions); face-validity-checked; feeds NF3. 🌿 GIT (do this FIRST, before touching any file): `git checkout dev && git pull && git checkout -b <story-branch>`; commit + push to THAT branch, hand back a PR → `dev`. ⛔ NEVER work on / commit to `dev`/`main` directly, ⛔ NEVER `git add -A`; re-check `git branch --show-current` before each commit (a parallel session shares the checkout); any `--publish`/`deploy.sh` is a POST-MERGE operator step. (CLAUDE.md 🌿.)

Match rate 97–98% of each ADP sample. ⚠️ **THE UNMATCHED NAMES ARE A DIAGNOSTIC, NOT NOISE — auditing all 6 (across all 8 samples, not one board) split into TWO causes:** (a) `kenny gainwell` was a **nickname-alias JOIN BUG** silently costing a genuinely draftable RB (18 g / 221 PPR in 2025) his ADP — fixed by an `adp_source._NAME_ALIASES` entry + a regression test; (b) the other 5 are **projection-universe absences, root-caused into defect #7 below** (the base-season inner-join anchor deletes any player who missed the whole base season — Aiyuk/Dell/Brooks/Lloyd — plus rookie `carson beck`). The audit that finds these: normalize every ADP name, diff against the projection's own (name, position) set, and index the projection by SURNAME so an alias miss is distinguishable from a true absence. Rookies DO get ADP (16 matched) because the join is on the NORMALIZED NAME, not gsis — a gsis crosswalk would drop exactly the rookies a drafter most wants ADP for. FFC is best-effort: a fetch failure logs a warning and the column renders "—" rather than failing the boards. ⏭️ follow-up story NF3.1 (player pages) queued below.

(b) Projections gains **pagination** (25/50/100/All, default 50) + a **Confidence tooltip** (it is a HISTORY measure — ≥10 base-season games = high, 5–9 medium, <5 low, every rookie low — explicitly *not* a quality measure). (c) **"Δ" renamed "vs ADP"** on Rankings AND League Board (Δ demands notation literacy). (d) **Rankings TIERS** via the optimizer's own `assignTiers`, so a tier means one thing product-wide. ⚠️ **Tiering the full 716 is BROKEN and looked plausible** — `assignTiers` splits on gap > mean+k·std, and the long flat tail collapses that threshold: 36 tiers with ranks 1–7 each alone. Restricted to the **above-replacement pool (~84)** the same k=1.0 gives ~10 genuine tiers, and it is the right definition (below replacement there is no value to group). Sub-replacement players are untiered. (e) **League Board now NAMES the replacement-level player** per position (QB13 Caleb Williams, RB34 Rachaad White, WR28 Alec Pierce, TE13 Hunter Henry at half-PPR/12 — the off-by-one is exact: 12 startable ⇒ the 13th IS replacement), turning an abstract baseline into a yardstick. (f) **MOBILE SWEEP** across 375/390/768 × all 4 fantasy surfaces: zero body-level horizontal overflow (tables scroll only inside their own containers). ⚠️ The sweep surfaced a real defect the eye would miss: **the new tooltips were HOVER-ONLY and therefore DEAD ON TOUCH.** Radix `Tooltip` closes on pointerdown BY DESIGN, so tap can never open it — `InfoTip` is now built on **Popover** (tap/click) plus hover handlers for mouse. ▶ NF3.4 — FEATURE-IMPORTANCE TRANSPARENCY (surface the model's own drivers per projection on the player page)   [App · 🧭 SONNET (small — the models are already fitted) · from NF3.1 2026-08-01]   ✅ DONE 2026-08-01
✅ **DONE 2026-08-01 — SHIPPED (PR pushed; live S3 already carries the per-player contributions; frontend renders on merge/deploy). Went BEYOND the spec's "global-per-position" floor to GENUINE PER-PLAYER attribution.**
  • ⭐ **THE PIVOT (operator caught it):** the first pass built position-level importance (the spec's floor) → operator flagged "we want what about a PLAYER contributes to HIS ranking." Reworked to genuine per-player attribution via LightGBM `pred_contrib=True` (exact TreeSHAP, NO new dep) — contributions sum EXACTLY to that player's own prediction. This RESOLVES the spec's honest-labelling crux by doing the harder thing (real per-player SHAP, not "what the RB model weights"). The position-level report survives as a research artifact, no longer surfaced.
  • ⚠️ **SCOPING (an honesty constraint, PM-relevant):** the numbers are from NF1 (the validated research model), NOT the served MVP-1 (`season_projection.py`, still what ships — "MVP-1 stands"). NF1's total can legitimately differ from the number elsewhere on the page → every payload carries `model_version`/`generated_at` and the UI frames it as "a separate research-model read," never implying it equals the served number.

▶ NF-FASTPATH = ⚡ DRAFT-SEASON MVP-1 — 2026 SEASON PROJECTIONS (the draft-tool content foundation + the operator's 8/22 deliverable)   [Model/product · 🧭 OPUS · ⏰ draft-season · off-box/SF-free/parallel-safe]

Veteran = a **3-yr recency+games-weighted** per-game line (0.6^age×games — regresses career/injured years toward the player's own baseline; fixes single-season recency bias that ranked Trevor Lawrence QB2 off a fluke 9-rush-TD 2025 → now QB5) shrunk by sample × an EXPECTED-GAMES role estimate (fixes `mart_projections_preseason`'s backup-QB-at-#1 — Malik Willis was its #1); rookies = draft-slot fp-curve × P1A residual (composite-first, bounded). **🐛 FIXED A SEASON-DATA CORRUPTION first** (see landmine): `dim_player` name-variant dupes fanned `fct_player_week` ×N (Achane 36-game/2× seasons) + the depth-chart-anchored spine dropped the entire 2025 rookie class (Jeanty/Egbuka absent) — cured (dim_player unique on player_id; fct anchored on the box score; guards `assert_nfl_fct_player_week_unique_grain`/`assert_nfl_fct_covers_stat_lines`). Report: `ablation_results/nf_fastpath_season_projection.md`. **🧬 2025 DEPTH-CHART FEED RESHAPE FIXED (2026-07-24):** the "lake lacks 2025" gap was nflverse RESHAPING the `depth_charts` feed (≤2024 weekly game_type/formation rows → 2025+ daily ESPN `pos_abb`/`pos_rank` snapshots); the old `stg_nfl_depth_charts` silently dropped the whole new schema. (`depth_charts_2026.parquet` maps to 0 until the 2026 schedule lands → re-ingest then.) **⏭️ MVP-2/NF-C1 OUTPUT-SCHEMA CONTRACT** (season totals, gsis_id grain): `proj_games`; passing `proj_pass_{att,cmp,yds,td,int}`; rushing `proj_rush_{att,yds,td}`; receiving `proj_{targets,rec,rec_yds,rec_td}`; `proj_fumbles_lost`, `proj_two_pt`(NULL); convenience `proj_fp_{std,half,ppr}` (NF-C1 RESCORES the raw line — do NOT consume these as the league score); uncertainty `fp_ppr_{sd,p10,p90}`, `uncertainty_type`(empirical=vet / parameter=rookie); meta `sport,projection_season,base_season,player_id,player_name,position,team_id,source,is_rookie,draft_overall,confidence,model_version,generated_at`.
🐛 **PROJECTION DEFECTS SURFACED BY MVP-3 DRAFT-TESTING (operator, 2026-07-25) — route to NF-D1/NF-D2/NF1; NOT draft-tool bugs (the optimizer ranks correctly off whatever the projection says):**
 1. **Rookie `team_id` is 100% NULL** in the projection (74/74) — the MVP-3 export now backfills it best-effort from `nflverse_players.latest_team` so the APP is correct, but the DURABLE fix is to carry the drafted team through the projection itself: `nflverse_draft_picks` only reaches 2025 in the lake (no 2026 draft yet), so NF-D1's roll-forward should re-ingest the 2026 draft-picks feed and set rookie `team_id` in `season_projection.project_rookies` (currently hardcoded `np.nan`).
 2b. ⭐ **ROOKIE 80% BANDS ARE CLASS-LEVEL, NOT PER-PLAYER — and the point projection can sit in their extreme tail (found 2026-07-29, NF3 UI review).** The operator flagged "Jeremiyah Love shows 0–443, that's not useful"; it is worse than wide, it is **not his band at all**. The rookie leg emits bands QUANTISED TO ~3 BUCKETS PER POSITION: all 74 rookies carry one of 3 QB / 3 RB / 3 TE / 3 WR bands, versus **600 distinct bands across 642 veterans** (genuinely per-player). Consequence: every rookie QB in the top bucket carries the identical **26.5–277.0** while their point projections span **25.1 → 268.3**, so `Fernando Mendoza` (268.3) sits in the top 3% of "his own" interval and a 25-point rookie sits at the bottom of the same one. A single band cannot centre on every player it is pasted onto. ⚠️ **This is the CLAUDE.md §0.5 selection-metric landmine in the wild** — NF1.4 tuned the rookie band on COVERAGE (.680→.790) and a position-constant band can hit nominal coverage while carrying ZERO per-player information. **Any refit must gate on SHARPNESS/per-player discrimination, with coverage only as a floor — never coverage as the target.** ⏭️ NF1.4 refit; the app needs no change once bands become per-player. **APP MITIGATION SHIPPED (not a fix):** the served label is now "Class-level" (never "Calibrated" — that would imply a per-player interval we do not have), rookie bands render muted with a `class` marker + explainer, and `export_draft_board_json.audit_interval_quality()` emits an ALERT-tier warning on every export (currently: "150/716 players carry a band shared with at least one other player… 2 players have a point projection in the extreme 5% tail of their own band"). That audit is the DQ check that would have caught this at build time.
 2. **Rookie OVER-valuation** — the draft-slot fp-curve is too hot: `Fernando Mendoza` (rookie QB) projects QB9 (268 pts, above Herbert/Mahomes); `Jordyn Tyson`/`Jeremiyah Love` sit top-tier. The P1A-residual + slot-curve needs a stronger shrink/prior (NF-D2 rookie priors; combine athletic profile).
 3. **Veteran QB ordering is off** — `Joe Burrow` QB≈19 (206), `Jayden Daniels` far too low (190), `Justin Herbert` (265) above Mahomes. QB fantasy points need the NF-D2 usage/context features (rush volume, team environment) + likely an availability/injury prior.
 4. **Missed-time vets under-projected** — `Omarion Hampton` (137), `Rashee Rice` (135) rank low (games-played/regression dragging them); revisit the expected-games × recency shrink for players with an injury-shortened base season.
 5. **No aging curve** — `Stefon Diggs` (168) rated too high for age; add an age/decline term (NF-D2 — combine/age features).
 6. **`Travis Hunter` ≈10 pts (WR262)** — almost certainly a role/usage or id-join miss for a two-way rookie; audit his feature row.
 7. ✅✅ **FIXED + SHIPPED 2026-07-29 (NF-D11 — see the story below).** The rescue is live: base-season-absent-but-still-rostered players are anchored on their most-recent PLAYED season and discounted by a §0.5-selected return-from-absence prior (board 716 → 777; Brooks RB153, Aiyuk WR112, Dell WR95, Lloyd RB141, all `confidence = low` with honest 0-floored bands), retired players still excluded, and the ADP-vs-universe coverage audit now runs on every board build. Root cause as diagnosed ↓. ✅ **ROOT-CAUSED 2026-07-29 (NF3 ADP audit) — `Jonathon Brooks` et al. MISSING = the BASE-SEASON ANCHOR cannot tell "retired" from "injured all year".** `run_season_projection.load_base_season` blends a 3-yr recency×games window, then **`weighted.join(base[...], how="inner")`** where `base = per_season[per_season.season == base_season]`, and the window SQL already drops a 0-game season (`having count_if(g) > 0`). So **a player who missed the ENTIRE base season has no base row → the inner join deletes him**, even though the window holds real production and the model could project him. The anchor's stated purpose (exclude retired/out-of-league players the multi-year window would sweep in) is sound; the side effect is not — a whole-season injury is indistinguishable from retirement. **Full 2026 casualty list, found by auditing every ADP name against the projection: `Brandon Aiyuk` (2023: 16 g / 249 PPR — a WR1 season), `Tank Dell` (productive 2023 AND 2024), `Jonathon Brooks`, `Marshawn Lloyd` — all 0 games in 2025.** ⚠️ These are exactly the high-variance names the PUBLIC IS DRAFTING (all four appear in the FFC ADP sample), so our board has no row at all for players a user will be asked to draft. Also missing: `Carson Beck` (2026 rookie QB absent from the NCAAF P1A rookie parquet — a separate rookie-coverage miss, not this anchor). **FIX BELONGS IN THE MODEL, NOT THE APP** (role/team/durability/sd are all read off the base season, so admitting a base-season-absent player needs a decided fallback — most-recent-played-season role + an availability prior — which is a projection-universe change, not a serving patch). Route to NF-D1/NF1; NF3 surfaces whatever the projection emits.
 8. ✅ **DUPLICATE player_id rows — FIXED AT SOURCE (2026-07-25).** The post-NF-D1 MVP-1 re-run emitted 721 rows / 716 unique: 5 low-value players (Taysom Hill/Heyward/Shorter/A.Williams/Westover) duped. ROOT: `run_season_projection.py`'s role join — `current_preseason` (from `stg_nfl_depth_charts_current`) wasn't deduped per player_id, so a multi-position depth-chart player fanned the `df.merge(role)`. CURE: `qualify row_number()` dedupe on `current_preseason` (keep best rank) + a **grain guard** in `build_projection` (`drop_duplicates(player_id)` keeping highest-fp + a loud warning if any drop). Re-ran the full chain (MVP-1 `--s3` → MVP-2 `--from-lake --s3` → export `--from-lake`) → 716 unique everywhere, 0 board dupes. MVP-3's export/frontend dedupes stay as defense-in-depth. (Follow-up nicety: a dbt-level unique-grain test on `season_projections`.)

🎯 GOAL: a league-config + scorer + VOR engine that turns the raw stat line into league-specific fantasy points, value-over-replacement, and a cross-position ranked board — for any manually-entered format.
DO:
 1. **LEAGUE-CONFIG SCHEMA** — a declarative settings object: per-stat scoring (pass yd, pass TD [4 vs 6], INT, rush yd, rush TD, **reception [0 / 0.5 / 1 PPR + TE-premium]**, rec yd, rec TD, fumble-lost, 2pt; bonuses optional/deferred) + roster/starter slots (QB, RB, WR, TE, **FLEX**, **SUPERFLEX/OP**, K, DST, bench) + league size (N teams) + PPR variant. Ship sensible PRESETS (standard / half-PPR / full-PPR / superflex) + custom override.
 2. **SCORER** — apply the config to MVP-1's raw line → league points per player. Deterministic, transparent, UNIT-TESTED against hand-computed examples (a known stat line × a known ruleset = a known score).
 3. **VOR / POSITIONAL SCARCITY** (the layer that makes cross-position ranking meaningful): replacement level per position = the "last startable" player's points, derived from league DEMAND = `N_teams × (dedicated starter slots + that position's expected share of FLEX/superflex)`. ⭐ **FLEX/superflex allocation is the subtle part — flex is filled by the best remaining RB/WR/TE (and QB under superflex) league-wide, so compute replacement AFTER allocating flex demand, not from dedicated slots alone.** VOR = points − replacement; rank overall by VOR. ⚠️ **superflex MUST visibly lift QBs** (QBs become flex-eligible → many more QBs started → QB replacement drops → QB VOR jumps) — that's the face-validity check the scarcity math is right.
 4. **UNCERTAINTY PASSTHROUGH** — carry MVP-1's `fp_ppr_{sd,p10,p90}` into the league score (rescale by the scoring transform, or rescore the stat-line quantiles) → an honest interval on VOR, not a false-precise point.
 5. **MANUAL FORMAT ENTRY** — a format selector (presets + custom); NO platform import yet (that's MVP-4/NF-C0, which auto-POPULATES this same config object → the config schema is the shared contract).
 6. **OUTPUT** — a per-league scored + ranked board (player, position, league_points, VOR, positional_rank, overall_rank, interval) to S3 + a readable ranked output. **This is MVP-3's (draft optimizer) input contract.**
⭐ **SHARED-ENGINE (operator 2026-07-22): the config schema + scorer + VOR math are SPORT-AGNOSTIC → this IS the NF-C1 / MLB F-C1 engine — build ONCE, instantiate per sport** (baseball reuses it with baseball scoring/roster; only the projection INPUTS differ). Design the config object + scorer with the sport as a parameter, not NFL-hardcoded.

✅ NF1.6 — BASE projections for KICKER + DEFENSE (DST) — position-universe extension   [Model/projection · 🧭 OPUS · edge-independent projection product · ⚠️ NOT a reopening of the CLOSED NF1.1–1.5 refinement arc]   (NEW 2026-07-29, operator honest-product call · **✅ DONE + LIVE IN PROD 2026-07-30 — incl. 4 post-ship UI fixes + 2 ADP bugs, see POST-SHIP below**)
✅ **SHIPPED — 74 rows (32 DST + 42 K) for 2026; K/DST now RANK on all 14 board combos instead of rendering "not projected".** Report: `ablation_results/nf1_6_kdst_base_projection.md`.
  • **Model:** `nfl_fantasy_kdst_base_v1` — `kdst_source.py` (IO) + `kdst_projection.py` (pure) + `run_kdst_projection.py` (CLI/validation/landing). Every shrink is a MEASURED reliability, re-fitted in-fold each run, not a constant.
  • **⭐ THE DESIGN DECISION THAT MATTERS:** the points-allowed DISTRIBUTION is emitted as **expected-GAMES-per-bucket** over 9 buckets that are the common REFINEMENT of the ESPN + Yahoo tier edges. A per-game tier table is NOT linear in season points allowed (so a season total cannot be scored under it) but IS linear in `games × P(bucket)` ⇒ the existing sport-agnostic linear scorer reproduces ANY tier scheme EXACTLY, **no engine change, and NF-C0b needs no new plumbing** — only its own `per_stat` weights. Distribution is EMPIRICAL, not parametric: P(shutout)=0.0099 and a NegBin matched to the observed mean/variance puts ~1e-4 there, missing the single most valuable atom by 2 orders of magnitude.
  • **Coverage (the gate):** walk-forward over 15 held-out seasons, 795 rows — **DST 0.897 (31 rows slack), K 0.830 (14 rows slack)** vs the 0.80 FLOOR; pooled 0.857, IS80 129.6; beats BOTH degenerate anchors. ⭐ The first cut BREACHED both floors and the cause was structural, not tuning: the pooled ROW quantile ignores that the quantile itself MOVES season to season (K-starter ratio q10 ranges 0.31–0.94). Fix = shift each bound outward by `BAND_CLUSTER_Z=1.0 ×` the across-season SD (pre-registered BEFORE measuring whether it cleared) — a parameter-uncertainty widening, **reported with its cost (+0.76% interval score), not as a free lunch.** DST over-covers and is DELIBERATELY not sharpened toward nominal (E2.1-r/NF1.8).
  • **⭐ DECISION (the story's REQUIRED one): `run_interval_revalidation.py` EXTENDED** to own the K/DST floors (`--rebuild-kdst-panel`, `--only kdst`). All 3 populations green. ⚠️ Its breach RESPONSE differs by design: rookie/veteran were SELECTED by a §0.5 bake-off ⇒ re-select; K/DST is **reported, not selected** ⇒ **WIDEN honestly**, never move the floor.
  • **🚨 HONEST FINDINGS (the framing, not a caveat):** kicker make rate ρ=0.085 and FG att/g ρ=0.117 ⇒ near-random; def TDs/safeties/blocked kicks ρ≈0.01–0.13 ⇒ projected at the LEAGUE MEAN and said to be. Leg strength IS real (≥50yd att share ρ=0.429) ⇒ per-kicker distance mix. ⚠️ **PAT att ↔ team points is 0.948 CONTEMPORANEOUSLY but only ~0.38 against the FORWARD predictor the model actually gets** — quoting the 0.948 would be a train/serve inconsistency dressed as a finding; both are reported. Held-out rank ρ: DST 0.322, **K starters-only 0.231** (pooled K 0.651 is inflated by JOB STATUS, not skill — reported separately for exactly that reason).
  • **Landed:** `s3://credence-sports-lakehouse/nfl/fantasy/derived/kdst_projections/season=2026/`; folded into the league board + draft-board JSON + the NF3 Projections surface, every K/DST record carrying `lowPred`/`predNote`.
  • **Guards:** `betting_ml/tests/test_nf1_6_kdst_projection.py` (62 tests, 2.0s). ⚠️ One real latent trap pinned: a projection missing the MVP-1 contract ALIAS columns makes the engine silently emit a **DEGENERATE ZERO-WIDTH** band (not a null) — this actually happened mid-build.
🩹 **POST-SHIP (2026-07-30, all operator-caught, all BACKEND-INVISIBLE) — theme: correct data discarded/misrepresented at the LAST INCH, past every backend check.** FOUR UI ROUNDS (guards now in the Python fast gate, each verified to FIRE on the pre-fix source): (1) K/DST rendered NOWHERE — board carried 74 rows + JSON in prod, but the frontend hard-filtered them via `SKILL_POSITIONS` + no K/DST position tab. (2) the "Tier" BADGE read as a rating not a caveat ("Jake Bates · Tier" = tier-one asset, the opposite of its warning) → moved to prose. (3) the tier COLUMN still assigned K/DST tiers (DEN D/ST green T1) — misread + wrong on merits (DST field spans ~10 pts vs each row's ±47 → gap-tiering splits noise) → K/DST render `—`. (4) removed K/DST from the League Board replacement-level panel (every league starts exactly one off a near-flat pool → supports no decision). **TWO REAL ADP BUGS (operator asked why kicker ADPs were sparse):** (a) DST matched ZERO ADP while FFC published 19 — FFC "Denver Defense" vs our "DEN D/ST" → normalized, no shared token → matched nothing, and it looked identical to honest undrafted blanks → now joined on TEAM CODE (incl. SEA ~pick 87). (b) ⭐ **the "vs ADP" delta was wrong on EVERY position tab for EVERY position since the column shipped** — ADP is an OVERALL pick number, a position tab ranks 1..n WITHIN position; subtracting gave Jake Bates +131 / Josh Allen +26 on rows where our board and the room AGREE exactly. The fake delta scales with draft slot → K/DST merely EXPOSED a latent QB/RB/WR/TE bug → both surfaces now rank ADP within-position first. (Sparse kicker ADP is NOT a bug — FFC publishes only drafted players; a blank honestly = waiver-wire.) → **2 durable lessons in sport_data_platform.md.**
👀 **OPEN ITEMS (routed):** (1) BYE-WEEK clustering in the draft optimizer is skill-positions-only (stacking your K's bye with your QB's is a real minor problem; excluded because the fix changes existing optimizer behaviour beyond scope) → **PRODUCT CALL** → **✅ RESOLVED — WON'T DO (operator decision 2026-08-03): leave K/DST OUT of bye-week clustering PERMANENTLY.** RATIONALE: K and DST are the most STREAMABLE / waiver-wire positions — a drafter who stacks their K's (or DST's) bye with another player simply streams a replacement that week at ~zero cost, so a DRAFT-TIME penalty optimizes against a problem that is trivially fixable in-season, and would only distort the board (penalizing a good-value K/DST for a non-problem). The bye-clustering penalty stays skill-positions-only by design. NF1.6b CLOSED (won't-do); do NOT re-open without a new product rationale. (2) NF-C0b remains the downstream scoring consumer for the points-allowed TIERS (never blocked NF1.6 — raw buckets emitted; it turns them into per-league tier scoring). (3) ⚠️ the residual `[ALERT] interval quality` on export = 784 OFFENSIVE rows (K/DST produce ZERO findings); the recap calls them "MVP-1 rookie class-level bands," but **NF1.7/1.8/1.9 shipped per-player rookie + veteran bands — this observation likely PREDATES those merges → RE-VERIFY the [ALERT] source once all four are live.** (4) ⚠️ **PRODUCT RISK = expectation-setting: these ranks are honest but WEAK by construction (held-out ρ ~0.32 DST / ~0.23 startable K); every surface now says so in prose. Any future request to make K/DST look more CONFIDENT (tiers, tighter ranges, a sharper badge) is the one to PUSH BACK on — it was tried twice this session and misread as a rating both times.**
🎯 WHY: the tools currently project OFFENSIVE SKILL only (QB/RB/WR/TE); K + DST slots render EMPTY ("not projected"). Users EXPECT to see kicker + defense projections even though those positions are low-signal and league inclusion varies. Ship at least a BASE model so K/DST are rankable + fill draft slots — with HONEST framing about their low predictability (do NOT over-claim precision). This is the honest completeness move, not an edge play.
🟢 **READY** (the MVP-1 pipeline + nflverse K/DST stats exist). Not deadline-bound. Edge-independent (projection product — face-validity/coverage gate, NO `best_alpha`/PBO; this is NOT betting).
🎯 GOAL: a BASE 2026 season projection for every draft-relevant K and DST, emitting the RAW components NF-C1 scores per league (mirror MVP-1's raw-line philosophy so ANY league's scoring — incl. NF-C0b's DST points-allowed TIERS + distance-bucketed FG — can score it), with HONEST wide uncertainty.
🔬 DO:
 1. K + DST historical stat lines from nflverse (team-defense box scores + kicker FG/PAT logs; derive from `fct_player_week` / the team-week feeds). Confirm coverage.
 2. **DST base model:** project the RAW DST components — sacks, INT, fumble-rec, DEF/ST TD, safety, blocked-kick, and the POINTS-ALLOWED distribution (needed for the tier scoring) — from team defensive quality (prior-year DST + team-def metrics, REGRESSED) + environment (Vegas team totals / schedule strength). DST carries MODEST year-over-year signal (good units persist) → project TIERS, not false precision.
 3. **K base model:** project FG made + attempts (+ an honest distance MIX — likely league-average unless a kicker shows real distance signal) + PAT, driven mainly by TEAM SCORING ENVIRONMENT (good offense → more PAT + FG chances) + a HEAVILY-REGRESSED kicker make-rate (kicker accuracy is near-random year-to-year — say so).
 4. **HONEST UNCERTAINTY + FRAMING:** K/DST are among the LEAST predictable fantasy positions → WIDE intervals + a "low-predictability / streaming-tier guidance, not precise ranks" caveat surfaced WITH the projection. The value is COMPLETENESS + relative TIERING (better/worse situations), not precision. NULL/unknown kept NULL.
 5. Land to S3 in the MVP-1 projection lineage (a K/DST prefix) + a ranked output; VALIDATE face-validity (do the top projected DSTs/Ks sit on good defenses / high-scoring offenses?) + a coverage report.
⚠️ **INHERIT THE INTERVAL-COVERAGE DISCIPLINE THE PROGRAM NOW OWNS (NF1.7→1.8→1.9) — but do NOT over-engineer a base band into a full §0.5 bake-off.** Three concrete NON-NEGOTIABLES, because K/DST bands are heavily SKEWED (both floor near 0 with a long right tail — more skewed than any offensive position): (a) **EMIT p10/p90 INDEPENDENTLY** through `SportProfile.base_p10_column/base_p90_column` (the asymmetric-band plumbing NF1.7/1.8 shipped) — do NOT carry a single symmetric `sd`, or the league rescore slides a skewed K/DST interval off its own point (the exact bug NF1.7 fixed for rookies); assert lo≤point≤hi. (b) **MEASURE coverage on the RIGHT population — LEFT-join realized outcomes, never `inner`+a games filter** (NF1.9, now in sport_data_platform.md): a cut kicker / a defense's zero-production week is exactly the tail the band must price; report coverage as a FLOOR (≥ nominal), NEVER a target to tune to (E2.1-r). (c) **REPORT coverage + width, don't bake-off it** — a BASE band ships with an HONEST reported coverage number, not a tuned one; if it under-covers, widen honestly rather than re-selecting. ⭐ **DECISION REQUIRED:** either EXTEND `run_interval_revalidation.py` (NF1.9's standing annual re-check) to cover K/DST floors, or EXPLICITLY scope K/DST out of it in the report — do not leave two new positions with silently-unmonitored bands (that unmonitored-floor gap is precisely what let the veteran band go 5 stories unmeasured). ⚠️ NF-C0b is a SCORING consumer, not a blocker — NF1.6 emits raw components (incl. the points-allowed DISTRIBUTION) so the tier scheme buckets them later; do not gate NF1.6 on NF-C0b.
🔗 FEEDS: NF-C1 scoring (raw components → league points, incl. NF-C0b's DST tiers + distance FG); the board/rankings + MVP-3 draft optimizer (K/DST slots now FILL, not empty); NF3 surfaces (K/DST rankable). ⭐ **upgrades the K/DST handling in NF-C0b / MVP-3 / NF3 from "not projected / empty" → "base-projected (honest wide bands)."**
🔒 HONEST FRAME: a projection product — edge-independent (no `best_alpha`/PBO; that's the betting posture); gate = face-validity + coverage + honest uncertainty. A BASE model DELIBERATELY — do NOT over-engineer a low-signal position; the win is completeness + honest tiering, not precision.
