✅✅ **DONE 2026-07-29 — CODE-COMPLETE, gates GREEN, board rebuilt + export DRY-RUN verified. ⏭️ 2 operator steps to reach users: `--publish` the boards + deploy the frontend.** **SHIPPED = `qreg α0.01 · sdgain 0`** — a linear QUANTILE REGRESSION (pinball, τ=0.10/0.90) of the realized rookie season on the story's named drivers (log point projection, log draft slot, the P1A residual sd, position), i.e. **the direct-learned FOIL beat all three non-parametric families.** 44 configs × 7 held-out draft classes (2019–2025): held-out **interval score 181.16 vs the class-level incumbent's 202.14 (−10.4%)** at coverage **0.808** (floor 0.80); **PBO 0.029 / spread 27% = LOW-PBO + WIDE-spread ⇒ a genuinely separated winner**. Defect metrics: distinct-band fraction **0.184 → 0.989** (live board: 81 rookies **12 → 80** distinct bands), point-in-the-extreme-5%-tail **30/553 → 0**, and **`audit_interval_quality()` is GREEN** on the rebuilt export. Rookie POINT projection **byte-identical** (asserted). ⭐ **THE WINNING BAND IS *WIDER* THAN THE ONE IT REPLACES** (127.7 vs 124.4 mean PPR, ≈2× a veteran's) — the win came from putting width where it belongs per player, not from shrinking it: sharpness was the SELECTION metric, honesty the CONSTRAINT. Operator's example now reads **Jeremiyah Love 15–346 around a 208 point (was 10–245, which pinned a 208 projection at the 96th percentile of "its own" band)**.
🧨 **FIVE THINGS THIS STORY LEARNED THE HARD WAY (all now guarded):** (1) **A BAND MUST BE CONDITIONED ON THE *SERVED* POINT, and re-deriving that point is a trap** — the first cut recomputed `slot curve × nudge, clipped` and was wrong by up to **3.3 PPR** because the served point is that target MINUS the fumbles term; the harness's point-invariance assertion caught it, and the cure is to DELEGATE to `project_rookies` (`rookie_point_projection`). (2) ⭐ **AN ORACLE THAT FAILS TO FIT MAKES THE ORACLE CHECK VACUOUSLY TRUE** — the production fitter refuses a per-position fit under 40 rows and a held-out class is only ~80 rows across 4 positions, so BOTH oracle attempts silently returned None and `oracle_respected` passed **on nothing**; the harness now RAISES on a missing anchor. (3) **THE ORACLE FLOOR IS SAME-FAMILY *AND* SAME-SAMPLE** — measured: a peeking MISSPECIFIED oracle was legitimately beaten by an honest well-specified candidate (215.5 vs 218.0), and a peeking k-NN's CAPACITY depends on sample size (k=60 of 62 rows is a constant band, k=60 of 650 is local) so an in-fold arm beat its own oracle 214.9 vs 262.2. "Peeking can only help" holds only at equal family AND equal resolution; the unit test uses a **permutation** (truth vs shuffled outcomes) instead, which is well-posed at any n. (4) **A "WIDEN-ONLY" KNOB MUST ACTUALLY BE ONE** — the P1A-sd widener was written two-sided (`exp(gain·z)`), so it SHARPENED half the field off a parameter-uncertainty z and cost coverage 0.808→0.773; clamped to `max(z,0)` it became monotone and every `sdgain>0` arm then *improved* coverage. (5) **THE GUARD ITSELF WAS UNGREENABLE** — `audit_interval_quality` fired on ANY band shared by 2 players, which is a rounding coincidence (two bench vets whose p10 both floor at 0), not a defect; it now tests the invariant that matters (a shared band whose members' points span >25% of its width cannot be centred on all of them) with the threshold **measured, not chosen**: class-level groups sit at 0.68–0.84, coincidences top out at 0.196.
⚠️ **HONEST TENSION RECORDED, NOT SMOOTHED (report §4b):** the top 3 configs sit within **0.2%** of each other, and `sdgain` 0.1/0.2 buy real coverage headroom (0.841/0.850 vs 0.808) for ~nothing on the primary metric. **We ship the pre-registered winner anyway** — "prefer more headroom above the floor" is MONOTONE IN WIDENING, so the `max_width` degenerate wins it outright; re-picking a tie on a criterion a degenerate wins is the E2.1-r inversion facing the other way. ⚠️ **STILL OPEN (disclosed, not fixed): QB coverage is 0.739 — below nominal in BOTH arms.** The floor was pre-registered POOLED and the rookie-QB cohort is ~10/class with ~35% never taking a snap, so the per-position estimate is thin; the shipped band does not make QB worse, it inherits the miss. A **per-position floor** is the honest next iteration. **ALSO SHIPPED:** the league scorer no longer re-symmetrises the band (it carried a single `sd` through the rescore, which slides a skewed rookie interval off its own point) — each side is now carried through independently via new `SportProfile.base_p10_column/base_p90_column`, verified across formats (lo/pts + hi/pts ratios identical; veterans stay symmetric on the CV fallback). Frontend: 2 map entries + 2 explainer strings so the "Class-level" tag stops lying (the class-level copy stays live for the thin-history fallback). 4 anchors held: oracle respected, zero-width / max-width / const-width / incumbent all beaten. Report: `ablation_results/nf1_7_rookie_intervals.md`. (Original prompt ↓.)

▶ NF1.8 — ROOKIE intervals: PER-POSITION coverage FLOOR (the honest NF1.7 follow-up)   [Model · 🧭 OPUS · 🔬 §0.5 · edge-independent · from NF1.7 2026-07-29]
✅✅ **DONE 2026-07-29 (CODE-COMPLETE, gates green; board re-export + publish is the operator's step).** SELECTED: **`qreg_sqrt+cqr[pos,add] · sdgain 0`** — NF1.7's quantile regression moved to the √ scale, plus a **MONDRIAN (per-position) CONFORMALIZED-quantile-regression calibration layer**. 80 configs × 7 held-out draft classes (553 rookie-seasons). **EVERY POSITION NOW CLEARS THE FULL NOMINAL 0.80 FLOOR** (Tier 1; the pre-registered Tier-2 QB relaxation was NOT needed): QB **0.741→0.815**, RB 0.777→**0.804**, TE 0.870→**0.900**, WR 0.826→**0.835**. Cost = **+1.3% interval score** (180.99→183.41), still **9.1% better than the NF1.4 class-level incumbent** (201.76) at a distinct-band fraction of 0.89 vs 0.18. Rookie POINT projection **byte-identical** (verified end-to-end on the 2026 class: max |Δ| = 0 on `proj_fp_ppr` AND every projected stat column); `audit_interval_quality()` **GREEN**; 23 new tests, all 8+5 mutation-verified to FIRE on broken code.
⭐ **THE MECHANISM, AND ITS FOIL PROVES IT:** the pre-registered POOLED-conformal foil is a numerical **NO-OP** (183.451→183.451, QB 0.778→0.778) because the base band's AGGREGATE out-of-fold coverage is already ≈ nominal — only reading the conformity scores **WITHIN a position** makes the QB shortfall visible. On the served 2026 class the fitted inflation is **+0.82 PPR at QB and exactly 0.0 at RB/TE/WR**: the layer touches ONE position. The QB gain splits ~evenly between the √ scale (0.741→0.778) and the Mondrian layer (0.778→0.815). ⚠️ The OTHER pre-registered per-position mechanism — a separate quantile REGRESSION per position — **LOST**, making QB WORSE (0.716): separate slopes overfit at QB's in-fold n. **Per-position CALIBRATION works where per-position FITTING does not.**
🧨 **METHOD LESSONS (all guarded):** (a) ⭐ **A PER-POSITION FLOOR CANNOT BE A MEAN OF PER-CLASS MEANS** — NF1.7's convention needed ≥3 rows for a position to enter a class's mean, so a thin position was silently DROPPED and the floor would protect a different population than it names; NF1.8 pools over ROWS (both conventions reported). (b) ⭐ **`max_width` SATISFIES EVERY PER-POSITION FLOOR and still loses** — that is the proof the floor stayed a CONSTRAINT and did not become a target, and it is why the floor is NOT tightened above nominal 'for safety' (every notch above nominal moves the eligible set toward the degenerate). (c) ⭐ **PBO ALONE WAS UNREADABLE AND WOULD HAVE FORBIDDEN A TIE** — PBO(eligible) 0.514 with a 27% whole-field spread reads as 'genuine overfitting', but the field holds ~15 near-clones within 0.3% AND known-bad nulls 27% away, so the whole-field spread is the wrong discriminator; the **FLIP DISTRIBUTION** shows 12/35 in-sample halves won by the shipped arm and 11/35 by `knn_norm k80` — a TWO-ARM TIE ACROSS FAMILIES 0.94% apart ⇒ the NULL on 'which arm', with Bailey's performance **DEGRADATION 2.0%** and a top-quartile spread of 0.11%. The tie is broken on the program's own **DEFECT metric** (the runner-up reverts 4.9% of rookies to the class-level band, distinct-band 0.63 / worst-shared 9) — never on coverage headroom, which `max_width` wins. (d) **a check that runs on nothing passes on nothing, AGAIN** — the first cut of the per-group-scalar test filtered on `lo > 0`, which selects ZERO rows on a rookie population (q10 floors at 0 for nearly everybody), so a deliberate per-player mutation PASSED it; cured by measuring the HI side + asserting the check was non-vacuous + a constructed-model test pinning BOTH bounds. (e) the NF1.7 reference arm is PINNED as a literal — deriving it from the production constants NF1.8 changes would silently compare NF1.8 against ITSELF and print 0.00 everywhere.
🚨 **DISCLOSED, NOT BURIED — the floor is met with a THIN margin.** Expressed in ROOKIE-SEASONS rather than coverage decimals: slack QB **1** row, RB **0** rows, TE 10, WR 7. QB's entire fix is 6 more covered rookie-seasons out of 81, and at 81 rows a hard nominal floor rejects a PERFECTLY-calibrated arm ~50% of the time (exact binomial; that rate barely moves with n — n buys detection of a smaller shortfall, not a lower false-reject rate). NF1.7's 0.741 was **not significantly below nominal** (1.3 SE), and neither was RB's 0.777 — so the floor is partly selecting on noise, which is exactly why every config is deflated and why the Tier-2 fallback was pre-registered. Also: the conformal arms calibrate IN-FOLD and therefore CANNOT see a next-class gap — positive evidence the QB gap is class-to-class variation rather than a fittable miscalibration. **RB is now the tightest position; a future class that moves RB is the one to watch.** Report `ablation_results/nf1_8_rookie_perposition_floor.{md,json}`; harness `run_rookie_perposition_ablation.py`; changelog 1 item wk 2026-07-27. ✅ **SHIPPED + LIVE 2026-07-29** — operator re-ran the projection and published 16 files to `s3://credence-prod-s3-api-cache/fantasy/nfl/2026/` (dry-run reviewed first; the NF-D12 banner fired). Served board: rookie mean band 125.4 → **130.8** PPR, rookie QB 145.6 → **152.1**, **80 of 81 distinct rookie bands**, no `[ALERT] interval quality` line (audit GREEN), NF1.4 rookie face-validity **pass**, ADP coverage 100% across 8 samples. ⭐ **VETERAN bands UNCHANGED — 0 of 703** (p10/p90/tier), so the change is surgically rookie-only, and the served point is identical to 2 decimal places for all 784 players. Frontend deploy still needed for the changelog item.
⚙️ **VERIFICATION NOTE (found during the operator's re-export, 2026-07-29) — DO NOT ASSERT BYTE-EQUALITY ON A POINT PROJECTION ACROSS TWO SEPARATE RUNS.** The handoff said the pre/post `proj_fp_ppr` diff "must be 0.0"; the operator measured **5.97e-13** and it looked like a defect. It is not, and the cause is worth recording because the same false alarm will fire on any future re-export or promotion check: **`np.polyfit` (LAPACK lstsq) returns ULP-different coefficients when the SAME rows arrive in a different ORDER** (measured: 3.55e-15 on the rookie slot curve's log-log coefficients), and a DuckDB read without a total `ORDER BY` is not order-stable across processes. Through `exp(a)·slot^b` and the stat-line rescale that becomes ~1e-13 PPR. **Two runs of byte-identical code differ by 8.5e-14, so the residue is process-level float noise, not a code change.** The band-config question was settled the right way instead — IN ONE PROCESS on the real data, NF1.7's config vs NF1.8's gives a point diff of **exactly 0.0** (and the in-repo guard `test_the_point_projection_is_IDENTICAL_across_every_band_configuration` pins that with `check_exact=True`). ⇒ **For a cross-run check, assert `< 1e-9` PPR, or compare the ROUNDED served values** (here: the served point is identical to 2 dp for all 784 players). ⭐ The check that actually proves NF1.8 was surgical is a different one and should be the one run in future: **VETERAN bands must be UNCHANGED — 0 of 703 p10/p90/tier changed**, while 81 of 81 rookie bands moved.
 (Original prompt ↓.)

Not a gate miss (the floor was pooled), but a PER-POSITION floor is the honest next iteration.

Keep the rookie POINT projection byte-identical. ⚠️ small-N per position (~10-35 rookies) → report power; a per-position floor may honestly WIDEN QB (structural availability uncertainty), which is correct, not a defect.

▶ NF1.9 — VETERAN interval coverage validation + per-position floor (the 90%-of-the-board gap the NF1.4→1.8 arc never touched)   [Model · 🧭 OPUS · 🔬 §0.5 · edge-independent · from NF1.8 2026-07-29]   ✅✅ DONE + SHIPPED + LIVE (2026-07-29) — `knn_norm k300`, board RE-EXPORTED to prod (all 784 rows `calibrated_per_player`)
✅ **RESULT — THE BAND WAS BROKEN, BADLY, AND IS NOW FIXED.** Measured for the first time over 13 held-out target seasons / **8,398 veteran-seasons**: the served normal approximation covers **0.545** of its nominal 0.80 and misses on BOTH tails (0.272 below p10, 0.183 above p90). Shipped replacement = `knn_norm k300 · sdgain 0` (per-player neighbourhood quantiles in position-normalised prediction space): **IS80 205.96 → 160.89 (−21.9%)**, pooled coverage **0.545 → 0.890**, every constrained position clearing the nominal floor with real margin (QB 0.682→0.858 / 64 rows slack, RB 0.549→0.887 / 174, TE 0.536→0.888 / 159, WR 0.511→0.907 / 337; FB unconstrained at n<400, 0.415→0.860). Tail split 0.272/0.183 → 0.025/0.085. Deflation: **PBO(eligible) 0.0** over 1,716 balanced season splits, Bailey degradation 0.21%, contender spread 1.43% vs a 28.0% whole-field spread ⇒ a genuinely SEPARATED winner (not a tie — the opposite of NF1.8's read). All four anchor guards pass; the veteran POINT is unchanged (board rebuild band-ON vs band-OFF: max |Δ| **8.5e-13**, zero drift on every other column). Report: `ablation_results/nf1_9_veteran_perposition_floor.md`. Bake-off wall time **~5m20s** (75 configs × 13 seasons, cross-conformal included); the annual re-validation is **4.8s**.
🧨 **METHOD LESSONS (all guarded):** (a) ⭐ **THE POPULATION IS THE WHOLE BALLGAME** — every other veteran backtest here joins realized `how="inner"` + `g >= 6`, which is right for a RANK read and FATAL for an interval read (it drops exactly the injury/release seasons the band exists to price). The panel LEFT-joins and scores a projected veteran who never played as a real **0**; **31% of veteran-seasons are exactly 0**. (b) ⭐ **COVERAGE IS STRUCTURALLY NON-BINDING ON THIS POPULATION** — with a 31% point mass at 0 and a bound floored at 0, the left tail is nearly un-missable (below-p10 0.025 vs nominal 0.10; 83% of lower bounds ARE 0), so coverage ≈ 1 − P(y>p90) ≈ 0.89 for ANY honest band and a 0.80 coverage TARGET would be INVERTED — hitting it requires deliberately under-covering the right tail. The peeking oracle itself covers 0.873. The E2.1-r rule in a NEW population; the pre-registered `normal_cov` arm quantifies the cost of the naive fix (+7.2% IS80). (c) ⭐ **THE CONFORMAL LAYER (NF1.8's WINNER) IS A MATHEMATICAL NO-OP HERE** — ~29% of conformity scores are EXACTLY 0 (a zero-outcome row whose bound floored at 0), so the (1−α) conformity quantile is pinned at 0 and all four CQR arms scored byte-identically to their base. A mechanism that cannot act is a finding, not an omission. (d) ⭐ **A PEEKING ORACLE IS A FLOOR ONLY AT MATCHED n, AND NF1.9 OPERATIONALISED IT** — the winner BEATS the peeking same-family oracle (160.9 vs 179.8) because the oracle can only fit the ~646-row held-out season while the winner trains on ~7,236. Rather than wave at NF1.7's lesson, the harness adds `matched_n_candidate` (the winner's own arm trained on ONE prior season) — the oracle beats THAT (179.8 ≤ 182.9), so the floor is respected at equal family AND equal resolution, and THAT is what gates. (e) **the PARAMETRIC foil lost by 2–10%** — a single linear conditional q10 cannot express a target that is genuinely 0 for the bottom eight projection deciles and clearly POSITIVE in the top one (empirical q10 83.8 PPR); a neighbourhood quantile handles the point mass natively. `knn_pos` (no position-invariance assumption) is 0.7% behind and inside the tie band, so that assumption is NOT load-bearing. (f) **anchor guard 1 FIRED FOR REAL** on the first full run — the oracle refused a fit for a thin position and returned nothing, which would have made its check vacuous; cured by making the oracle DEGRADE to the normal band exactly as a candidate does. (g) **the second permutation anchor must be a DIFFERENT FAMILY** from the winner's, or it is a byte-identical duplicate that adds nothing (it was, until fixed).
🩹 **TWO DEFECTS FOUND BY NF1.9's OWN GUARDS, both fixed:** (1) ⭐ **A TRAIN/SERVE SKEW IN THE BAND'S MOST IMPORTANT FEATURE** — `project_veterans` emits `fp_ppr_sd` ROUNDED to 2dp for display but feeds the band the UNROUNDED season sd, so the first panel (which stored the rounded column) would have fitted the band on a systematically different feature than it is served with. Caught because the **served-band reproduction proof** disagreed by exactly one rounding step (0.1); cured with a `fp_ppr_sd_raw` handoff kept OUT of `OUTPUT_COLS`. (2) ⭐ **`np.floor(x*10)/10` IS NOT ALWAYS ≤ x** — when `x*10` rounds UP to an integer in float64 (`3.6999999999999997*10 == 37.0`) the "outward" rounding lands ABOVE x, so a band can marginally EXCLUDE its own point estimate — the exact incoherence the export guard fires on. Found because the `oracle_point` anchor (zero width AT the realized value) read 0.946 coverage where it must read 1.0. Fixed in the shared `_finish` contract AND in both served paths (veteran + rookie) with min/max clamps; `oracle_point` now reads 0.991, and **the residual 0.9% is exactly the negative-PPR seasons** — which is finding (3): **a fantasy season CAN be negative** (102 of 11,885 = 0.86%, min −7.28, 86 of them QBs), so the `lo ≥ 0` clip makes those rows uncoverable by construction. The clip is KEPT deliberately (a displayed negative p10 is worse for a drafter) and DISCLOSED as a bounded ceiling on achievable coverage.
📏 **EXPORT-GUARD UPDATE (`audit_interval_quality`):** a shared band must now span ≥ **17 PPR** (1 PPR/game over a 17-game season — a decision-resolution quantity fixed from the UNIT, not tuned to the result) as well as >25% of its own width. ⚠️ **A scale-FREE ratio has no traction when the scale is tiny** and that is a SECOND false-positive mode: 4 of 5 offender groups on the first NF1.9 board were deep-bench players whose ENTIRE SEASON projections spanned 1.3–4.7 PPR inside a 3.2–13.8-wide band (0.08 PPR/game). It is NOT a silencer — the NF1.9 board still trips it on 2 players, and the NF1.7 class-level defect (243 PPR of spread) clears the floor by 14×.
✅ **RE-EXPORT DONE — LIVE ON PROD (2026-07-29):** board re-exported via the guarded `--publish` flow; all **784 rows** now `calibrated_per_player`, mean band width **64.3 → 104.3**, p10=0 for 77.4% of the board, top-tier vets keep a real floor (Josh Allen 130.7–447.0). ⏭️ **STANDING OPERATOR TASK:** run `run_interval_revalidation.py` ONCE A SEASON after the completed season lands (4.8s; exits non-zero on a per-position floor breach → re-run THAT population's bake-off, never move the floor). ⚠️ Rookie **RB is the tightest floor in the program (0 rows slack)** — the likely first breach.
🎯 WHY: the ENTIRE NF1.4→1.7→1.8 arc validated intervals for **81 ROOKIES**. The **703 VETERANS (90% of the board) carry point ± 1.2816·season_sd — a NORMAL approximation off game-to-game variance (`uncertainty_type="empirical"`), NEVER coverage-validated** (no veteran interval-coverage number exists in ANY ablation report). ⚠️ a normal approx on a RIGHT-SKEWED season total WITH injury risk is exactly the shape that UNDER-COVERS the left tail — and the NF-D11 returning-from-absence players already show p10 pinned at 0.0. Same defect class NF1.4 found in rookies, UNEXAMINED, on 9× the population.
🧭 STEP 0 — LOCATE + CONFIRM THE REUSE (do this first; it sets the whole plan): (a) find the VETERAN band construction — grep for `uncertainty_type` / `empirical` / `1.2816` / `season_sd` in the projection code (`run_season_projection.py` and the interval/band helper it calls); that site is what NF1.9 replaces IF coverage fails. (b) find the NF1.8 ABLATION HARNESS + its report — the rookie interval-score/per-position-floor bake-off script + `ablation_results/*NF1.8*` (or the NF1.7/1.8 report the session names); NF1.9 SWAPS the population into that harness, so confirm the harness parametrizes population rather than hard-coding rookies (if it hard-codes, the first task is to lift the population to an argument — that is expected, not scope creep). (c) confirm the four anchor guards + the flip-distribution/Bailey-degradation/contender-spread deflation read are importable functions in that harness, not prose in the report — if they're prose, port them as code. Report what you found (paths + whether the harness is population-parametrized) BEFORE running the bake-off, so a genuinely-well-covered veteran band is a fast NULL (measure → floor passes → refit unnecessary → ship the measurement + the standing re-validation, no new band) rather than a forced refit.
🔬 DO (§0.5): apply the NF1.8 harness to the VETERAN population (swap the population; KEEP the interval-score selection + per-position coverage FLOOR + the four anchor guards + the flip-distribution/Bailey-degradation/contender-spread deflation read). Measure veteran interval coverage per position; IF it under-covers (esp. left tail / skewed seasons / returning-from-absence) → refit to a per-player skew-aware band (CQR-style, not the normal approx) selected on the interval score with a per-position floor. Keep the POINT projection unchanged — ⚠️ assert `< 1e-9`, NOT byte-equality (the np.polyfit-ULP / DuckDB-order-stability lesson from NF1.8), or compare veteran bands-unchanged.
⭐ ALSO establish the STANDING ANNUAL RE-VALIDATION: when a new class's outcomes land, re-run the ablation (~21s) for BOTH rookie + veteran floors → a floor breach = a re-selection TRIGGER, not silent degradation (a per-position floor breach is INVISIBLE at serving time — coverage needs realized outcomes). ⚠️ RB is the TIGHTEST floor now (0 rows slack) — the position a future class breaks first.
🔒 edge-independent (no `best_alpha`; gate = per-position coverage floor + interval-score sharpness). NO app change (re-export via the guarded `--publish` flow).
GATE/AC: veteran interval coverage measured per position; refit to a per-player skew-aware band with a per-position floor IF it under-covers (interval-score-selected, deflated, anchor-guarded, points unchanged <1e-9); a standing annual re-validation established for rookie + veteran floors. 🌿 GIT (do this FIRST, before touching any file): `git checkout dev && git pull && git checkout -b <story-branch>`; commit + push to THAT branch, hand back a PR → `dev`. ⛔ NEVER work on / commit to `dev`/`main` directly, ⛔ NEVER `git add -A`; re-check `git branch --show-current` before each commit (a parallel session shares the checkout); any `--publish`/`deploy.sh` is a POST-MERGE operator step. ▶ NF-D13 — AUDIT NF-D3's competitor scorecard for the AGGREGATE-vs-MEMBER defect (E7.11 defect #4 verbatim)   [Data/correctness · 🧭 SONNET · from E7.11 2026-07-29 · ⚠️ potential LIVE bug]
✅ **DONE 2026-08-01 — CLEAN NULL, no fix needed.** Traced `benchmark_scorecard.py` (`_score_pair`/`_disagreement`/`build_scorecard`) line by line against all three sub-defects. **Structurally immune, not merely lucky:** NF-D3's scorer never builds an N-source consensus column the way E7.11's prospect board did (`consensus_rank = mean(sources)`) — every competitor system (ADP/ECR/ESPN/Sleeper/FF-file) is graded against us **independently, pairwise, one at a time**. Concretely: (1) `n_sources < 2` rows are structurally impossible — each `m` is an inner join of `base` (us) with exactly ONE system, so both sides are present by construction on every row, never a bare 1-source row; (2) "us" is never inside the thing it's compared to — every system is loaded from its own independent pipeline (FFC/FantasyPros/Rotowire/ESPN/operator CSV), never blended with our own projection first, so there is no self-comparison; (3) the disagreement z-scores (`zf`/`zs` in `_disagreement`) are computed over the IDENTICAL merged population `d` for both sides — no mismatched percentile denominators, because there's only ever one population per pairwise comparison. All three defect variants require an aggregate-of-members step to exist first, and it doesn't here. **Numbers UNCHANGED** (nothing to fix) — the honest fade claim stands exactly as reported: ADP fade ρ **0.478 (us) vs 0.247 (system)**, per `ablation_results/nf_d3_benchmark_scorecard.md` (generated 2026-07-26, still current). Shipped: a regression test (`test_scorecard_systems_score_independently_of_each_other` in `betting_ml/tests/test_nfl_fantasy_projection.py`) that pins a system's own grade as byte-identical regardless of what OTHER systems are registered — the concrete invariant that makes the defect impossible — plus an inline audit note at `benchmark_scorecard.py::_disagreement` so a FUTURE change that folds multiple competitor systems into one blended "consensus" entrant is flagged to re-apply the E7.11 discipline (n_sources≥2, exclude-self, shared residual) before wiring it in. **Unblocks NF3.2's NF-D13 gate** (NF1.5b remains). Fast gate green (907/907 `football` shard).
➕ **SCOPE EXTENSION (same session, 2026-08-01): onboarded REAL ESPN POINT-PROJECTION data (2025 completed + 2026 in-progress) — and caught TWO loader bugs before they became a false headline.** The first pass showed us beating ESPN by a huge margin on 2025 → recognised as a RED FLAG, not a win (the implausible-win discipline), and traced to two real parse defects: (1) ESPN's export carries **TWO rows per player** (a season-total AND a per-game-average line) that were being blended into one ranking, corrupting both sides' scores; (2) ESPN's position labels in the raw file are **shifted relative to their own internal ID scheme**, silently DROPPING every QB and WR from the comparison (only RB/TE were ever scored). Both fixed + regression-tested; fast gate 4,074 green. **CORRECTED RESULT — the honest, broadened benchmark:** against EVERY system we can score (ADP, FantasyPros ECR, Sleeper, ESPN rank, ESPN real point projections, Fantasy Footballers) our within-position ordering is **competitive but NOT superior** — we trail slightly across the board (Δρ ≈ −0.01 to −0.16; **Sleeper is the toughest**). Against ESPN's real point projections on the one full completed season (2025) we are a **statistical dead heat (Δρ = −0.014)** — not an artificial win, not an embarrassing loss. ⭐ **The ONE reproducible edge stands: high-conviction ADP fades — our side of our strongest disagreements predicts realized outcome 0.478 vs ADP's 0.247.** ⇒ **the defensible public claim = "competitive with public consensus overall, and meaningfully outperforms the MARKET on our highest-conviction contrarian calls"; the claim we CANNOT make = "we beat the consensus."** This is exactly the NF3.2 receipts frame. ▶ NF-D16 — PRE-REGISTERED rookie-point LEVEL RECALIBRATION (RB/TE/WR) — NF1.4's documented COLD bias, corrected as a per-position CONSTANT   [Model · 🧭 OPUS · 🔬 §0.5 · edge-independent · ⚖️ PM RULING 3 off NF-D15 2026-08-01 — the HIGHER-PRIORITY next modeling pull]
   ✅⏸️ **DONE 2026-08-01 — MODEL RATIFIED, PUBLISH HELD. ⚖️ PM RULING (option C): hold the publish, flip serving OFF, and re-specify the tripped PLACEMENT clause under its OWN pre-registration before re-reading the board — "gated on validating the clause, not on the board passing an unvalidated one."** The recalibration cleared every gate it pre-registered and is **not** in question; what is held is one ADVISORY clause. ⭐ **NF-D17 (the pre-registered re-specification) IS DONE, AND IT CAME BACK THE OTHER WAY: the incumbent placement clause is VALIDATED, not mis-specified.** Statistic/reference/threshold were fixed in writing first — the rank-space mirror of NF1.4's LEVEL fix (cap = **Q10** of the realized per-class BEST rookie's OVERALL rank, since ranks are better when smaller; reference = §3b's `(15, 13, 13, 52, 10, 7, 29)`). Result: cap **Q10 = 8.8** vs the incumbent's 10 — **about ONE board slot**, and reality breaches "no rookie in the overall top 10" in **2 of 7 seasons (29%)**, statistically indistinguishable from the corrected LEVEL clause's own **9/28 (32%)**. So unlike its sibling (whose first cut fired 7/7 and carried zero information until re-anchored), placement was approximately right all along — **§3c's "the unvalidated half is probably mis-calibrated too" premise is REFUTED by measurement.** ⇒ **the 2026 board's rookie RB at overall rank 6 breaches an HONESTLY-CALIBRATED bar = a GENUINE VETO; the hold STANDS.** ⭐ **AND THE VERDICT IS THRESHOLD-INVARIANT, which is what makes it un-reverse-engineerable:** rank 6 breaches at **every** quantile in Q05–Q25 (caps 7.90 / 8.80 / 9.70 / 10.60 / 11.50) **and against the most permissive reading available — the observed MINIMUM realized rank, 7.** Rank 6 sits outside the reference's ENTIRE observed support, so no threshold choice inside any defensible band could have produced this answer (the same "the answer must not rest on a gate level I chose" discipline the story already applied to its DSR). ⚠️ **DISCLOSED: rank 6 was already known when the clause was derived** — blindness was impossible, so the insulation is structural instead: the derivation is a mechanical transcription of NF1.4's template with NO free parameter, and the verdict is invariant across the whole band. ⏭️ **NEXT:** any "attenuate the correction at the TOP of the board" idea is a **NEW pre-registered hypothesis, never a λ re-pick** (§6 stands: the λ grid was pre-registered, λ=1 was selected, and the slot-varying `mult_tier` was already INELIGIBLE for breaking do-no-ordering-harm — so "attenuate at the top" is not free). Re-enabling serving is ONE line (`recal_hist=_rookie_full` in `run_season_projection`). ⚠️ **RECORD THE STRUCTURAL FINDING SO IT IS NOT MIS-CITED (PM-required): the recalibration REORDERS ROOKIES ACROSS POSITIONS** — the rookie #1 flipped **QB → RB** (Mendoza 268.3 @12 → Love 291.6 @6, Love moving 33 → 6) precisely because the correction lifts RB/TE/WR and, by pre-registration, does **not** lift QB. **NF-D16's "moves no ranks" argument was always a WITHIN-POSITION claim**; it is not a claim about the merged draft board, and that is exactly how a level change reaches a placement gate. ───── Original session record follows. **The FIRST rookie-point change this program has validated, and the first NF fantasy MODEL win after five deflated nulls (NF1/NF1.1/NF1.2/NF1.4/NF-D15).** The pre-registered pooled test selects `ols_slope · λ 1` — a per-position AFFINE level recalibration — moving the pooled draftable-tier MAE **1.0738 → 0.9407** over 7 held-out draft classes, **positive in ALL 7**, PBO **0.029**, whole-field DSR **0.996** (gate ≥0.95), one-sided paired **p=0.0033** vs α=0.10. Per position: **RB 80.43 → 72.30, TE 53.92 → 42.29, WR 68.84 → 65.33** PPR. ⭐ **THE MECHANISM SIGNATURE CONFIRMS IT IS A LEVEL EFFECT — the exact test NF-D15's arm FAILED:** the winner moves pooled bias **−20.87 → −5.41**, i.e. TOWARD zero (NF-D15's per-player arm moved it FURTHER, −20.87 → −22.44). The incumbent's measured tier bias reproduces NF1.4's documented defect exactly (RB −57.69 / TE −44.45 / WR −48.81 vs NF1.4's −58/−44/−49). 🚨 **THE MOTIVATION IS NOT THE RESULT:** NF-D15's `p=0.0052` best-of-33 observation is cited ONCE as prior motivation and never as evidence; the clean pre-registration made the effect *larger*, not smaller, and under a framing fixed in writing BEFORE the run. ⭐ **THE FRAMING WAS PRE-REGISTERED AS POOLED, WITH ITS REASON, AND IT DID NOT DECIDE THE ANSWER:** a LEVEL effect is a-priori COMMON across positions (NF1.4's bias is the same sign and comparable magnitude at all three) and the ship UNIT is the whole per-position vector — one change to `project_rookies` that applies at RB/TE/WR together or not at all. The DISCLOSED per-position reading agrees: **RB and TE survive the 3-test BH-FDR independently** (p 0.0128/0.0259), WR does not (0.1785) — the mirror of NF-D15, which pre-registered per-position and disclosed pooled. Sensitivity: it also ships at DSR≥0.0, with the DSR removed entirely, and on the contender reading. 🧨 **METHOD LESSONS (guarded, reusable):** (a) ⭐⭐ **A PEEKING CEILING MUST BE PER *FORM*, NOT ONE FOR THE FIELD — this story's FIRST CUT SHIPPED THE BUG AND THE FIX IS A REGRESSION TEST.** It floored every arm on the per-position CONSTANT ceiling; but `mult_tier` and `ols_slope` each CONTAIN that constant as a special case, so either can legitimately beat it — a CAPACITY effect (NF1.7 (b) / NF1.9 (f)), and the check would have vetoed a REAL result as a metric inversion. Now each arm is floored by the peeking version of its OWN form, and the four ceilings order exactly as capacity predicts (**ols 0.7784 < tier 0.8354 < const 0.9091 < offset 0.9107**) — an internal-consistency check the single-ceiling design could not produce. (b) ⭐ **A PERMUTATION ANCHOR IS NEAR-VACUOUS AGAINST A *LEVEL* HYPOTHESIS, AND THE HONEST MOVE IS TO PROVE IT RATHER THAN PRESENT A NEAR-TIE AS A PASSED TEST.** A level is a MARGINAL statistic, so a WITHIN-position permutation preserves it — for the additive form EXACTLY (`mean(y)−mean(point)`, measured max |Δ| **7.1e-15**). So BOTH permutations are registered: `permuted_within` is declared in advance as an expected TIE (1.0949) and `permuted_across` is the one that must be beaten (1.1247). *A mechanism that cannot act is a finding, not an omission* (NF1.9). (c) ⭐ **THE STORY'S OWN 'ZERO ORDERING HARM BY CONSTRUCTION' CLAIM WAS PARTLY WRONG, AND MEASURING IT IS WHAT CAUGHT THAT.** `ols_slope` is only *CONDITIONALLY* monotone — an affine inverts a whole position's board when its slope is negative — so its measured zero rank movement is a property of THIS RUN'S fitted slopes (all positive, 0.666–1.460), not of the form. Three ordering classes are now distinguished (by-construction / conditional / reordering) and only `mult_tier` genuinely reorders — **which is exactly why it is INELIGIBLE at every λ, and why a field of only constants would have left the do-no-ordering-harm constraint passing having examined nothing.** (d) ⭐ **THE RISK ARGUMENT ('a level shift moves no ranks') IS TRUE WITHIN A POSITION AND FALSE ACROSS THE BOARD** — rookies and veterans share ONE board, so lifting every rookie moves them UP against veterans. NF1.4's face-validity gate owns that risk and **IT IS TWO-SIDED**: the COLD incumbent breaches its level cap in **0 of 28** cohort-positions while REALITY breaches **9 of 28**, so 'zero breaches' is the SYMPTOM, not the target, and reality's rate is the reference. Measured: the candidate still breaches **0/28** ⇒ not over-corrected (if anything still conservative). ⚠️ Added AFTER the run cleared its pre-registered gate and DISCLOSED as such — admissible in exactly one direction, because it can only VETO a ship, never enable one. ▶ NF-D22 — A POWER-DERIVED FALLBACK COVERAGE FLOOR for structurally-thin interval groups (NF1.8 prescribed it and left it unspecified)   [Model/method · 🧭 OPUS · 🔬 §0.5-adjacent (it changes a GATE, not a model) · edge-independent · ⏳ **POST-LAUNCH — deliberately NOT launch-gating** · from the NF-D21 disposition, PM 2026-08-05]   🔵 **NOT STARTED — and ⛔ DO NOT PULL IT EARLY TO UNBLOCK ANYTHING**
🎯 **WHY:** NF1.8 established that a hard point-estimate coverage floor at nominal is **not a valid gate for a structurally-thin group** — at n≈81 it rejects a PERFECTLY-calibrated arm ~50% of the time, *and that false-reject rate barely moves with n* (more data buys the power to detect a SMALLER true shortfall, not a lower false-reject rate). It explicitly anticipated a **power-derived fallback floor** and left it unspecified. NF-D21 then hit exactly the predicted case: rookie **RB** (n=148) missed the 0.80 floor by **2 covered rows**, on a gate whose own false-reject rate there is **0.500**. So the instrument, not the result, is the defect — and rookie RB has been shipping with **ZERO rows of slack** at the served λ=0 too, i.e. it is one row from breaching *with or without* any recalibration.
🚨🚨 **THE DISCIPLINE IS THE ENTIRE STORY — ⛔ NON-NEGOTIABLE, and if you cannot hold it, DO NOT RUN THIS STORY.** Deriving a floor while a known breach sits on the table looks exactly like reverse-engineering a floor from the answer. It is admissible ONLY under all four:
  1. ⛔ **Derived from `n` and a PRE-STATED false-reject target ONLY.** ZERO reference to the 0.7905 measured in NF-D21 — not as motivation, not as a sanity check, not as a "does it clear?" confirmation. Write the derivation, review it, and defend it on that basis alone. (`n` is a DESIGN quantity, known before any result exists — that is the whole reason it is the admissible input.)
  2. ⛔ **Apply it to ALL structurally-thin groups**, not just the one that failed. A floor that changes for exactly the group that breached is not a floor.
  3. ⛔ **NF-D21 is explicitly OUT OF SCOPE.** Land this on its own merits, POST-LAUNCH, temporally separated from that breach.
  4. ⛔ **Re-gating NF-D21 against the resulting floor is a DOWNSTREAM CONSEQUENCE, never this story's motivation.** It will *likely* clear at λ=0.5 afterwards — which is precisely why the motivation must be independent of that fact. ⚠️ If you find yourself checking whether the derived floor lets NF-D21 through, you have already broken rule 1.
🧭 **WHY IT IS CLOSED, NOT PENDING (read this before treating NF-D21 as blocked-on-you):** the PM CLOSED NF-D21 rather than parking it *because* "a dangling open-pending-the-floor-fix is exactly the pressure that would bias the floor toward clearing λ=0.5". ⇒ **nothing is waiting on this story.** If a future session tells you NF-D21 is blocked on NF-D22, that framing is itself the bias this design removed — correct it.
🔬 DO: (1) derive the fallback floor from `n` + a pre-stated false-reject target (the natural form: the largest floor whose `P(reject | truly nominal)` under the class-CLUSTERED SE — rows inside a draft class are NOT independent draws (NF1.8) — stays at or below the target); (2) state the target and its justification BEFORE computing any group's floor; (3) define "structurally thin" as a rule over `n`, not a list of groups; (4) apply to every interval family the program owns (rookie NF1.8 / veteran NF1.9 / K-DST NF1.6) and REPORT which groups it changes; (5) prove the derived floor is STRICTLY WEAKER than nominal only where the power argument justifies it, and never above nominal (NF1.8: every notch above nominal moves the eligible set toward the `max_width` degenerate); (6) keep the DEGENERATE anchors — `max_width` must still SATISFY the floor and still LOSE the metric (a constraint a degenerate satisfies is fine; a criterion a degenerate wins is fatal).
GATE/AC: the floor is a FUNCTION of `n` + a pre-stated false-reject target, with the derivation legible and containing no reference to any measured breach; it applies to all thin groups by rule; `max_width` still satisfies-and-loses; the report states which groups move and by how much. ⛔ NO publish, NO λ change, NO NF-D21 re-gate inside this story. GIT FIRST (worktree) + PR → `dev`.

▶ NF-D17 — TRACK-RECORD Δρ POPULATION-SENSITIVITY re-computation (is the shipped FFC-only receipts headline UNDERSTATED vs a matched population?)   [Model/analysis · 🧭 OPUS · 🔬 §0.5 (it touches a PUBLIC, COMPETITOR-RE-RUNNABLE claim → full rigor, NOT a quick re-compute) · edge-independent · from NF3.2 2026-08-02]   🟡 **DONE 2026-08-03 — RECORDED NULL ON THE PRE-REGISTERED QUESTION, AND IT POINTS THE OPPOSITE WAY FROM THE HYPOTHESIS. ⇒ KEEP THE SHIPPED NUMBER; the public page is UNTOUCHED, nothing published, no changelog.** The pre-registration (`track_record_population.py`) was committed in its OWN commit BEFORE the harness existed — populations, sources, metric, anchors, uncertainty rule and decision rule all fixed in writing first. ⭐ **THE PREMISE IS REFUTED BY MEASUREMENT: on the matched population the shipped number DOES NOT MOVE — P0 +0.022 vs P1 (our universe ∩ FFC ∩ MFL) +0.022, identical to three decimals.** The reason is structural and was knowable BEFORE any ρ was computed: **FFC’s ranked players are very nearly a SUBSET of MFL’s** (the intersection retains 159–172 of FFC’s 140–172 rows/season), so matching removes almost nobody and there is nothing for a population effect to act on. ⚠️ **AND THE SHIPPED +0.022’s OWN 90% PAIRED BOOTSTRAP INTERVAL IS [−0.006, +0.051] — IT INCLUDES ZERO.** On FFC’s top-~162 population our ordering is not distinguishable from the draft crowd’s, so the honest reading is that the claim is CAREFUL, not understated; the page’s existing "multi-season average, not a promise" caveat + the `_CLAIM_DENYLIST` are doing real work and must not be loosened. ⭐ **WHAT IS ACTUALLY POPULATION-SENSITIVE IS THE OTHER SOURCE, AND THAT IS THE FINDING WORTH KEEPING:** MFL reads **+0.173 [+0.140, +0.207]** (excludes zero, 7 seasons incl. 2025 which FFC has no archive for) on its own deeper ~264-player population but **collapses to +0.008 [−0.027, +0.040]** restricted to FFC’s shallower one ⇒ the FFC/MFL gap is a **DEPTH effect, not a source-quality effect** — hold the population fixed and the two real-draft crowds agree to within 0.014. A draft-crowd ordering degrades faster than ours as you go deeper into the pool. 🟡 **OPERATOR OPTION (NOT this session’s recommendation, and the pre-registered rule does not permit it):** a "+0.173 over ~264 players/season" framing is real and interval-clean, but it is a DIFFERENT claim, not a better measurement of the same one; quoting it without its depth would be the exact confound this story exists to prevent, and switching the headline source to the one that reads higher is the §4 prohibition. Honest form = report BOTH, each labelled with its population + depth. 🔎 **NEITHER DEFERRED FIGURE REPRODUCED under any admissible population** (+0.144 FFC: closest admissible = P0 +0.022, gap 0.122; +0.088 MFL: closest admissible = P1 +0.008, gap 0.080) — reported as a finding per the §7 pre-commitment, not chased. A DISCLOSED post-hoc probe confirms it is not the `g>=6` survivor filter either (`g>0`: +0.025 / +0.024). ✅ **ANCHORS ALL GREEN:** A4 reproduction EXACT (P0 = the live scorecard’s +0.022/6 seasons and +0.173/7 seasons), and A1 identity = 0.0 / A2 oracle floor dominates / A3 degenerate random loses on **57/57** population×source cells. 🧨 **METHOD LESSONS (guarded, reusable):** (a) ⭐⭐ **A ONE-SIDED DEPTH TRUNCATION MANUFACTURES AN ARBITRARY Δρ — measured at 0.20 wide here** (top-200 vs MFL: **+0.218** truncating by the SOURCE’s ordering vs **+0.016** truncating by OURS), an order of magnitude larger than the effect under study; any "top-N" benchmark comparison that does not say which side defined the N is uninterpretable, which is why both sides were pre-registered as mandatory. (b) ⭐ **THE FORENSIC LEG NEEDED ITS OWN ADMISSIBILITY RULE AND IT FIRED:** the closest cell of ANY kind to the deferred +0.088 is a one-sided depth reading at +0.079 (gap 0.009, INSIDE the ±0.02 match tolerance) — without the pre-registered inadmissibility of one-sided depth, that coincidence would have been published as a REPRODUCTION by a method the same document calls meaningless. **A near-match found by a method you already disqualified is a coincidence, not a corroboration.** (c) ⭐⭐ **A GUARD ON AN AND-COMPOSED RULE IS VACUOUS UNLESS ITS FIXTURE SATISFIES EVERY *OTHER* CLAUSE — this story’s most important guard shipped that way in its first cut.** The test named for the decision rule’s `excludes_zero` clause used the run’s real shape, but the *non-overlap* clause already refused it, so **deleting `excludes_zero` from the source left the suite GREEN**; found only by deliberately breaking the source, cured by a fixture where every other clause is SATISFIED (plus a mirror for the other clause). Generalises to any AND-gate in this repo. (d) ⭐ **A "matched population" fix does nothing when one population is already a SUBSET of the other — and the intersection COUNT is a design quantity available before any metric is computed.** Report the population overlap BEFORE the metric and this null was predictable. 🔒 `best_alpha = 0`; nothing published, no `--publish`, no `deploy.sh`, no changelog (no user-facing change), the live payload and headline are byte-unchanged. Generalises to EVERY AND-gate in the repo.** (c) a near-match found by a method you already DISQUALIFIED is a coincidence, not a corroboration (the closest cell to +0.088 was a one-sided depth reading at +0.079 — only the pre-written inadmissibility rule stopped it being published as a "reproduction"). (d) a "matched population" fix does NOTHING when one population is a SUBSET of the other, and the intersection count is a DESIGN quantity available before any metric — read the overlap FIRST. → the AND-gate lesson (b) added to CLAUDE.md. ⏭️ Worktree reclaim: `git worktree remove ../nf-d17-dp-population && git branch -d nf-d17-dp-population`. Per-position training sets are TINY (QB 33–68 rows) → deep nets overfit + lose, and we ALREADY emit calibrated, skewed predictive distributions via partial-pooling + NegBin/Beta-Binomial (the exact outcome MC-dropout / Bayesian-NN / TFP-SHASH chase) more sample-efficiently AND more explainably. ⚠️ DL also sits in TENSION with the transparency brand — a heavier, less-explainable model must clear a HIGHER bar than a lift, not just meet it.
🗂️ DISPOSITION of the scan items (what to test vs what's tracked elsewhere): (a) **MLP / tabular DL** → the only near-term-testable piece: fold in as ONE deflated candidate learner class in the NF1.5 bake-off (test honestly; likely null) — NOT a separate build; (b) **LSTM/RNN** → only relevant to a future WEEKLY/game-level model (no sequence in one-row-per-season draft projection) — revisit when a weekly model exists; (c) **GNN** (teammate/opponent graph) → elegant for H-CORR/H-SPILL but data-hungry + premature at our N; (d) **DRL draft agent (DQN/PPO)** → heavier + LESS explainable than a VOR + optimizer (transparency tension) — a VOR/greedy/MILP optimizer wins v1; (e) **transformer/multimodal sentiment** → that's NF-D9 (weekly-layer, heavy, fragile); (f) **MILP salary-cap optimizer** → NOT DL + genuinely actionable, but it's the draft/auction OPTIMIZER, tracked in NF-C5 / MVP-3, not here.

🔒 REVISIT the heavier pieces (LSTM/GNN/DRL) only when (a) a weekly/richer-data model exists AND (b) the cheaper feature levers (NF1.2 / NF-D8 / NF1.5) are exhausted.

✅✅ **SLICE 3 = SHIPPED (2026-07-26) — TEAM-CHANGE / DEPTH-JUMP OPPORTUNITY (the operator's "player enters a new team & takes a higher depth spot" ask).** For a player who CHANGES teams (base-season team ≠ projection-season team) at RB/WR/TE, the stale old-team per-game line under/over-states the new role → rescale the per-game line toward the NEW team's role-level volume (positional median fp/g at the new depth rank), blend 0.35, clamp [1/1.6, 1.6]. Diagnostic proof the signal is real: among team-changers `corr(depth-climb, next fp/g change) = +0.26`; climbers +1.3 fp/g, non-climbers −1.5. ⭐ **ABLATED (held-out within-pos ρ 2020–2025, via the SHIPPED `project_veterans` path): RB +0.008 · WR +0.006 · TE +0.007 · QB +0.000 (untouched — out of scope), and the MOVER subpopulation +~0.03** — every skill position up, net-positive on the full-board gate, robust over blend∈[0.30,0.40]. Leakage-safe (forward team+role from the freshest preseason depth snapshot; backtest uses proj-season weeks 1–3). ⭐ **ALSO FIXED a forward-role FRESHNESS bug: `load_base_season` read only the `season=base` depth partition (stale March snapshot) — now reads `season IN (base, base+1)` preferring the latest `snap_ts`, so the July-2026 post-FA/draft snapshot wins → 49 movers now fire on the 2026 board (was 1), with face-valid directions (Hollywood Brown KC→PHI buried −22; a backup moving into a lead role boosted).** ON by default; fires only where the depth feed has captured the move ⇒ re-run as offseason depth refreshes through camp. Files: `season_projection.py` (`role_volume_prior`, `mover_opportunity_scale`, `_MOVER_OPP_*`), `run_season_projection.py` (base_team/proj_team + freshness fix + role-vol prior), `run_team_context_ablation.py` (validator, calls the shipped path), `test_nfl_fantasy_projection.py` (+4 tests). Fast gate 1900✅.
✅✅ **SLICE 4 = SHIPPED (2026-07-26) — VEGAS TEAM ENVIRONMENT, QB (the operator's Vegas ask + QB-ordering complaint). LEAKAGE-SAFE via WEEK-1 lines — NO new ingest.** Key realization: the leakage wasn't Vegas per se, it was aggregating SEASON-LONG lines. A team's **WEEK-1 game line is set BEFORE any of the season's games** ⇒ leakage-safe, and it's a decent forward proxy for the season environment (corr ≈0.65). QB tilt on the projection-season team's Week-1 implied points (`dim_nfl_game.total_line/spread_line`; already in the lakehouse, full 2020–2025 + 2026 Week-1 all posted) → **held-out QB ρ +0.012** (2020–2025). GENTLE by design (`_ENV_TILT_BLEND=0.06`, clamp ±10%): a QB's own line already partly reflects his offense, so a big tilt would DOUBLE-COUNT — ρ is magnitude-insensitive so the gentle setting keeps ~85% of the lift while staying face-valid (Goff +28 not +60). QB-scoped (RB/WR/TE carry team context via their own usage line). ⭐ HEADROOM: the season-long (LEAKY) env is QB +0.06 = the ceiling → Week-1 captures ~1/5; a RICHER FORWARD signal (preseason win totals / a captured preseason line snapshot) would recover more (the operator's "get the data" ask — see the data-sourcing options in the session handoff). **SYSTEM FIT (archetype × scheme)** deferred to NF1 (larger build). Reproduce: `run_team_context_ablation.py` → `ablation_results/nf_d2_team_context_ablation.{md,json}`. ⭐ **STRATEGIC: slices 1, 3 & 4 ALL shipped from data ALREADY in the lakehouse — winning channels = ROLE/VOLUME (s1 snap-usage, s3 team-change) + cross-team ENVIRONMENT (s4 QB Week-1); s2 (efficiency) was the null. Remaining headroom = a richer forward-Vegas signal + joint weighting in NF1.**
✅ **SLICE 5 = SHIPPED (2026-07-26) — INJURY / AVAILABILITY (forward roster-status cap on expected games).** ⚠️ Two channels tested: (a) RETROSPECTIVE injury-report burden (weeks Out/Questionable last year) = weak/confounded (partial corr +0.067 WRONG sign — base games-played already captures durability; injury designations entangle with player quality) → NOT used; (b) FORWARD roster status = strong+clean. A projection-season roster flag of unavailability (RES/IR, PUP, NFI, SUS) set preseason ⇒ leakage-safe; empirically (players productive the prior year) RES→3.7 g, PUP→2.4, SUS→6.9 vs ACT→13.2. FIX: cap expected games toward the empirical status level (`_INJURY_STATUS_GAMES_CAP`, blend 0.7), rescale the line. THE OPERATOR'S CASE (offseason-surgery player misses first few games) = PUP/NFI. ⚠️ **The within-pos ρ lift is only +0.002 BECAUSE THE ρ EVAL FILTERS TO ≥6 REALIZED GAMES — which EXCLUDES the shelved players this fixes** (a season-ending-IR player plays 0 games) → the number badly UNDER-states it; this is a CORRECTNESS fix (don't rank an IR/PUP star as startable), not a ρ optimization. QB benefits most (+0.005). Wired into `project_veterans`; reads projection-season Week-1 roster status. **🚨 DATA-SOURCING (operator: "source elsewhere, it's critical"):** the nflverse injury REPORT is IN-SEASON ONLY (no offseason) and 2026 is UNPUBLISHED; the roster PUP/IR flag is the in-lakehouse forward source but LAGS to camp (only 4 flags on the 2026 board now). ⭐ **The Sleeper API is the recommended EARLIER/richer forward source — verified live: free, `injury_status` (60 PUP + 24 IR + 360 Questionable NOW, July 2026) + `injury_body_part` ("Knee - ACL", "Achilles") + `gsis_id` mapping (155 injured players joinable). A Sleeper ingest → COALESCE into `load_forward_roster_status` surfaces offseason-surgery cases NOW — a follow-on pipeline story (PM prompt in the session handoff).** Reproduce: `run_team_context_ablation.py` arm `D_injury_availability`.
✅ **SLICE 6 = DONE (2026-07-26) — ADP MARKET CONSENSUS as a FEATURE/PRIOR + the NF-D3 BENCHMARK. Result: a clean POSITION SPLIT; ships OFF as a blend, delivered as the benchmark asset + an optional QB/RB prior.** ⭐ **NEW FREE/NO-AUTH/REPRODUCIBLE SOURCE FOUND: Fantasy Football Calculator API** (`fantasyfootballcalculator.com/api/v1/adp/ppr?teams=12&year=Y`) — real-draft consensus (2,000–3,000+ drafts/yr), snapshotted the week before Week 1 ⇒ **LEAKAGE-SAFE**, `adp`/`stdev`/`high`/`low`/`times_drafted`, coverage **2018–2024 + live 2026** (2025 the lone gap; FantasyPros' table is JS/API-gated — 5-row preview only — so FFC is the source). Crosswalk to gsis by normalized (name, position) off `fct_player_week` = **98–100% of veterans** (91% for 2026, the miss = unplayed rookies). ⭐ **ABLATED 2019–2024 (`run_adp_ablation.py`, 3-way: model-only / +ADP@w / ADP-alone + a DISAGREEMENT panel) — the finding is a POSITION SPLIT, not one verdict:** (1) a GLOBAL blend is **net-negative on the board** (ADP covers only ~37% of the scored universe → blending disturbs covered-vs-uncovered order); (2) at **QB/RB the MARKET out-orders us** (covered-tier ρ QB 0.48 vs 0.33, RB 0.62 vs 0.52) and our fades are noise (QB fade ρ **−0.04 vs ADP 0.38**) — a box-score line structurally misses QB/RB order drivers (scheme, rushing role, committee, environment); (3) at **WR/TE OUR model wins** (ties/beats ADP on the covered tier AND on the fades: WR 0.44 vs 0.38, TE 0.31 vs 0.05), and — the headline for a non-market product — **where model & ADP most disagree the MODEL predicts the realized finish better overall (0.51 vs 0.28).** ⇒ **SHIP DECISION: `_ADP_PRIOR_BLEND = 0.0` (OFF).** This projection is a NON-MARKET product (roadmap §0 "market-blind for non-market models"); its edge IS the disagreements, so a blanket ADP blend would erase the exact within-tier WR/TE advantage NF-D2 exists to build. ADP is delivered as **(a) the NF-D3 benchmark asset** (`run_adp_ingest.py` → `nfl/fantasy/benchmarks/adp_benchmark`, season-partitioned Delta) and **(b) an OPTIONAL, evidence-backed QB/RB-scoped prior** (`blend_adp_prior(..., positions=('QB','RB'))`, applied within the ADP-covered tier — lifts covered QB +0.11 / RB +0.10) the operator can flip on as a product/market call. Files: `adp_source.py` (NEW — FFC fetch + normalize + gsis crosswalk, caches to `artifacts/adp_cache`), `season_projection.py` (`blend_adp_prior`, `_zscore`, `_ADP_PRIOR_BLEND=0.0`), `run_adp_ablation.py` (NEW), `run_adp_ingest.py` (NEW — the NF-D3 lake asset), `test_nfl_fantasy_projection.py` (+7), `ablation_results/nf_d2_adp_ablation.{md,json}`. Slim play frame CACHED per season (`artifacts/defense_plays_cache/`) + fitted strengths cached (`defense_strength_cache/`), assemble-once. (2) **roster-churn (NF-D1/NF-D2 mover pattern, DEFENSIVE side)** — last-season defensive SNAP shares (`snap_counts` S−1) ↔ this-season preseason ROSTER (`rosters` S) crosswalked on **pfr id**, split by position group (pass = secondary + edge; rush = front seven) → `returning_share` / `churn` + top losses/adds (face-valid: WAS lost Curl/Fuller & added Lattimore; TEN added Sneed/Diggs). ▶ NF-D7 — EXPECTED FANTASY POINTS (xFP) + TD REGRESSION (the #1 modeling technique we're missing)   [Data + model · 🧭 OPUS · 🔬 §0.5 · feeds NF1.1/NF1.2 · edge-independent]   (NEW 2026-07-27, from the deep-dive research)
✅✅ **DONE 2026-07-27 — construct BUILT + leakage-safe + PREMISE VALIDATED; the projection-blend ABLATION is a clean NULL (dropped, like the NGS slice-2), delivered as FEATURES for NF1.1/NF1.2.** Shipped `xfp_source.py` (NEW): reads raw nflverse PLAY-BY-PLAY (S3 Delta `nfl/raw/pbp`, `receiver/rusher_player_id` + `yardline_100` + `air_yards`/`cp`/`xyac_mean_yardage` + TD flags), buckets every carry/target by FIELD POSITION (gl≤2 / rz5 / rz10 / rz20 / ff), fits LEAKAGE-SAFE league TD-conversion rates on the base-season window (≤base — goal-line rush ≈0.50, end-zone/RZ target ≈0.29–0.44, midfield ≈0.005–0.02), and computes per-player window-weighted **xTD** (rush+rec), **xREC** (Σ catch-prob), **xRecYds** (Σ cp·(air_yards+xYAC)), **xFP/g**, **td_luck_ratio**. Opportunity read CACHED per season (`artifacts/xfp_cache/`, assemble-once). Pure `regress_td_rate` blends the base-season per-game TD rate toward the expected rate, wired into `season_projection.project_veterans` (`xfp_td_blend`, **default OFF `_XFP_TD_BLEND=0.0`**) + `run_season_projection.build_projection`. **① PREMISE VALIDATED (non-overlapping single seasons, 2,575 pairs 2020–2025): base-season EXPECTED-TD out-predicts base-season ACTUAL-TD for next year — rush ρ 0.665 vs 0.655, rec ρ 0.629 vs 0.590 (50/50 blend best for rec 0.634).** ② **PROJECTION ABLATION = NULL: every blend∈{0.25,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.75} is net-NEGATIVE on the top-tier (draftable, top-N/pos) pooled ρ (−0.004…−0.013 vs the full shipped MVP-1 baseline 0.395); oracle floor intact.** ③ **FEATURE best-shot (in-fold learned ridge on the xFP set, the NF1.1/NF1.2 lens) also NULL (−0.006 pooled).** WHY (mechanism — the slice-2 lesson): MVP-1's recency-weighted line + positional shrink ALREADY mean-revert the noisy TD spike, so a SECOND explicit opportunity-regression is redundant/double-regresses (and TDs are a fraction of PPR points, so a top-tier fp RE-ORDER barely moves). ⇒ **SHIP OFF (`_XFP_TD_BLEND=0.0`), per the NF-D gate (a null add is dropped, not shipped); xFP + expected-TD delivered as LEAKAGE-SAFE candidate FEATURES for NF1.1/NF1.2 via `xfp_source.load_xfp_features` (a learned per-position model may still weight them jointly — this proves only that a HEURISTIC blend doesn't earn its pipeline).** Files: `xfp_source.py` (NEW), `run_xfp_ablation.py` (NEW — premise + blend-sweep + feature best-shot, `--build-cache`), `season_projection.py` (`_XFP_TD_BLEND` + `xfp_td_blend` step), `run_season_projection.py` (xfp join + param thread), `betting_ml/tests/test_nfl_xfp.py` (NEW, 12 fast-gate tests). 🎯 WHY: the fantasy-analytics field's biggest projection lever is EXPECTED FANTASY POINTS (xFP) — the points a player "should" have scored given his OPPORTUNITY (targets, air yards, aDOT, end-zone/RED-ZONE touches, carries, weighted RZ opportunity), independent of actual outcome. It SEPARATES skill from luck: a player who out-scores his xFP got lucky (regress DOWN); one who under-scores with elite opportunity is a breakout/buy (regress UP). We project off RAW production + usage share — NOT an explicit xFP construct. ⭐ Pair with **TD REGRESSION** (TDs are the biggest + NOISIEST FP driver → regress TD rate to an EXPECTED-TD rate from RZ/end-zone opportunity; don't carry a lucky TD season forward). Research: xFP + TD-regression are the two most-cited stabilizers.
🔬 DO: compute a per-player-season **xFP** from opportunity (targets/air-yards/aDOT/RZ + carries/RZ-carries → expected pts per opportunity-type × volume) off nflverse/NGS play data; + an **expected-TD** rate from RZ/end-zone opportunity. Use them (a) as a REGRESSION prior (blend last-year actual toward xFP + expected-TD, weighted by stability) and (b) as candidate FEATURES for NF1.1/NF1.2. Leakage-safe as-of. §0.5 ablate the xFP-regressed projection vs the raw-production baseline on the TOP-TIER metric; deflate. `best_alpha=0`; a null add dropped.
📓 Read: the deep-dive (expected-points models + TD regression) + NF1/NF-D2 (usage) + NGS/PFR marts + §0.5.
GATE/AC: a per-player xFP + expected-TD construct (opportunity-based, leakage-safe); regressed projection ablated vs the raw baseline on the top-tier metric, deflated; survivors feed NF1.1/NF1.2. 🌿 GIT (do this FIRST, before touching any file): `git checkout dev && git pull && git checkout -b <story-branch>`; commit + push to THAT branch, hand back a PR → `dev`. ⛔ NEVER work on / commit to `dev`/`main` directly, ⛔ NEVER `git add -A`; re-check `git branch --show-current` before each commit (a parallel session shares the checkout); any `--publish`/`deploy.sh` is a POST-MERGE operator step. So "we out-projected X" FITS the honest-analytics identity — IF it's airtight.
🚨 **THE HONESTY BAR — a public "we beat X" claim MUST clear this or it backfires on the whole brand (a user/competitor re-runs it, finds it cherry-picked → credibility death for an honest-analytics product):**
 • **APPLES-TO-APPLES:** same player universe (state the top-N/pos or full pool), same scoring format, same realized-outcome truth, same metric — you cannot compare our full-pool ρ to their top-40 ρ.
 • **NO HINDSIGHT / NO LEAKAGE (the cardinal sin):** you cannot tune the model on 2025 then claim to "beat 2025" — the comparison MUST be genuine holdout (our projection built from ≤2024 data) or a pre-registered FORWARD test. This is the projection analog of the betting leakage discipline.
 • **SCOPE THE CLAIM TO WHAT'S TRUE — ⚠️ WE DO NOT BEAT FF TODAY:** MVP-1 LOSES within-tier RB/WR ordering to FF; we win TE + overall MAE + full-population ρ. So the honest CURRENT claim is narrow ("we beat FF on TE and overall accuracy"), NOT "we beat FF." The broad claim is EARNED only after NF-D2 closes the ordering gap AND NF-D3 proves it out-of-sample across ≥2 systems and ideally ≥2 seasons. **The claim depends on NF-D2 landing first — it is not free.**
 • **MULTI-SYSTEM + MULTI-SEASON:** one system, one season is an anecdote (could be their off year). A durable claim beats the CONSENSUS across several systems and holds across seasons.
 • **PUBLISH THE METHODOLOGY:** the strongest version links the reproducible scorecard (metric, universe, holdout) so the claim is self-verifying — honest-analytics as the moat, not marketing spin.
DO: ingest historical preseason projections/rankings as a `nfl/fantasy/benchmarks/` lake asset, keyed **player-season**: **FantasyPros ECR/consensus + historical ADP** (best multi-year coverage → start here) + where obtainable **PFF · ESPN · 4for4 · Football Outsiders**. Then extend `score_vs_realized` to emit "us vs each system" deltas automatically (per position + within-tier ρ + MAE). ⚠️ respect each source's terms/rate-limits; NULL a system where a season is unobtainable (don't fabricate).

We WIN **only** on our HIGH-CONVICTION FADES vs ADP (**0.48 vs 0.25**) and are **competitive at WR**. ⇒ the ONLY airtight public claim is NARROW: **"where we most disagree with consensus our picks out-predict the market, and we're competitive at WR"** — ⛔ NOT "we beat consensus on WR/TE," NOT "we beat the consensus," NOT "we beat the market." ▶ NF-C0b — MANUAL LEAGUE-SETTINGS EDITOR (the customization FLOOR — hand-enter/edit settings when platform import can't)   [App · 🧭 SONNET (an app settings-editor CRUD form over an existing config object) · frontend + backend · needs the NF-C1 config object (MVP-2 ✅) · complements NF-C0]   (NEW 2026-07-29, operator — a beta tester NOT in the operator's leagues; screenshots of a Sleeper league)   ✅ **DONE 2026-08-01 — SHIPPED + DEPLOYED (frontend + API + projection re-export).** The manual editor produces the SAME `fantasy_engine` `LeagueConfig` platform import produces (no second schema — imported + typed-in are the identical object). ⭐ HONEST-COVERAGE = three verbatim UI verdicts: **applied** (weight moves the number exactly) / **derived** (your league is finer than our projection → folded, labelled approximate when sub-values differ) / **captured** (stored faithfully, contributes nothing, and says so) — coverage is MECHANICAL (a term is "applied" only if a projection column actually exists; nothing self-promotes).  • **Special teams** (defense + player: ST-TD, forced/recovered fumble). Misc.
 • **Roster slots:** QB, RB, WR, TE, **FLEX = W/R/T** (support ≥2), **SUPERFLEX**, K, DEF, **BN** (n), **IR** (n). The screenshot = QB/RB×2/WR×2/TE/WRT×2/K/DEF + 5 BN + 3 IR.
⚠️ **HONEST COVERAGE (non-negotiable — do NOT fake what we don't project):** our PROJECTIONS are OFFENSIVE SKILL only (QB/RB/WR/TE). **K and DST scoring is CAPTURED for config fidelity but those positions are NOT projected → the ranked board ranks the projected positions; K/DST render as "not projected", never a fabricated value** (mirror MVP-3's already-shipped "K/DST-empty slots handled gracefully"). **Median scoring** (the screenshot's "second matchup vs league median") is a STANDINGS/SCHEDULE rule that does NOT affect per-player projections or VOR → RECOGNIZE + label it as a known league feature we don't apply to the board (a future matchup/standings-sim concern, not this story). Be explicit in the UI about what's applied vs captured-only.

▶ NF-C5 — AUCTION DRAFT SUPPORT (auction VALUES + a budget-aware live optimizer)   [App/backend + model · 🧭 OPUS · parallel to NF-C2 snake · ⭐ SPORT-AGNOSTIC — MLB roto auction reuses it]   (NEW 2026-07-25, operator-scoped)
🎯 WHY: auction leagues are a distinct, common format — redraft/SNAKE (NF-C2/MVP-3) is our PRIMARY, but auction is a first-class OPTION many serious players use, and it needs its OWN optimizer because the core dynamic (a fixed BUDGET + live price INFLATION) is nothing like a snake draft. ⭐ **build it SPORT-AGNOSTIC in `fantasy_engine`: auction is arguably MORE prevalent in fantasy BASEBALL (roto auction) than football → MLB `F-C` reuses this verbatim, same as the VOR engine (MVP-2).**
🧩 **PART A — AUCTION VALUES (extend NF-C1's config + `fantasy_engine`):** add `draft_type: snake|auction` + `auction_budget` (e.g. $200) to the `LeagueConfig`; emit DOLLAR VALUES — distribute the budget POOL above $1-minimums across positive-VOR players by VOR share (total $ = teams × budget; reserve $1 × total roster spots as minimums; allocate the surplus by each player's VOR share), per `(config, size, budget)`. Carry honest uncertainty as a value RANGE, not a false-precise $. Sport-agnostic → the same code produces MLB roto auction values.
🧩 **PART B — THE AUCTION OPTIMIZER (the "additional optimizer" — live, budget-aware):** given MY remaining budget, my roster slots (filled/open), the board (remaining players + values), and the MARKET (what's been spent) → recommend the bid. ⭐⭐ **INFLATION is the core dynamic a snake optimizer has NO analog for: as players go over/under value, the REMAINING pool inflates/deflates → recompute dynamically (remaining $ in the room ÷ remaining draftable value = the inflation multiplier).** Outputs: **(1) MAX BID per player** = min(inflation-adjusted value-based max, AFFORDABILITY cap = remaining budget − $1×open-slots-after-this) → **NEVER strand a roster spot with no money**; **(2) NOMINATION strategy** — nominate non-targets to drain others' budgets when you're flush; nominate targets when the market's soft; **(3)** positional-need + roster-construction under budget + "$X left for Y slots = $Z/slot" guidance. Live-recompute (fast).
⭐ **OUR EDGE:** values + max-bids come from OUR projections/VOR with a transparent "why this bid" (value → inflation-adjusted → your affordable max) + honest uncertainty — not just consensus AAV.
🖥️ APP-TARGET: `frontend/` (auction draft UI — budget tracker, live values, max-bid, nomination) + `fantasy_engine` (values); ⛔ never legacy Streamlit; `cat frontend/package.json` first. Edge-independent (no `best_alpha`).

Scope stayed Sleeper-only (lock 4) — no ESPN changes. 20 new tests (`test_nf_c0c_sleeper_player_names.py`) + the full existing NF-C0 suite (279) + fast gate (4288) all green; tsc clean on the touched file. Changelog entry added (2026-07-27 week, prepended).
🎯 WHY: NF-C0's Sleeper import works, but `GET /league/{id}/rosters` returns **player IDs only** (`["11599","5846",…]`) — no names. So an imported roster renders as a column of opaque numbers, and the import surface currently ships an honest warning saying so. Resolving them needs Sleeper's `/v1/players/nfl` dump, which Sleeper documents as **~5 MB and asks callers to fetch at most once per day** — far too heavy for a request path, and pulling it per-import would breach their own guidance. ⇒ a cached daily artifact is the fix.
⚠️ **THIS IS THE ONLY THING BLOCKING SEVERAL DOWNSTREAM FEATURES**, which is why it is worth doing before more import surface: roster-aware waiver/FA ranking (NF-C4; NF-C5 is auction), "who's on MY team" board highlighting (NF-C6), keeper/dynasty valuation of an imported roster, and any live-draft sync that must map a Sleeper pick to OUR projection row.
🔬 DO: (1) a daily job fetching `https://api.sleeper.app/v1/players/nfl` → a SLIM S3 artifact (`sleeper_player_id → {full_name, position, team, nfl_id/gsis_id where present}`) — drop the ~90% of fields we never read so the served blob is small; (2) an ID BRIDGE from `sleeper_player_id` to OUR projection player id. ⭐ **Sleeper's payload carries external ids (`gsis_id`, `espn_id`, `yahoo_id`, `sportradar_id`) — USE THOSE, do NOT name-match as the primary key** (the NF-D12 lesson: nflverse ships null/late `gsis_id` for rookies, so a name+position+team fallback is needed for exactly the rookies a draft board cares most about, and the fallback must REPORT its match rate rather than silently dropping). (3) the import surface renders names; the honest "IDs only" warning is REMOVED only where names actually resolved, and an unmatched player still shows honestly rather than being hidden.
🚨 APP-TARGET: UI = `frontend/` only; backend/read = `app/backend/`; ⛔ never Streamlit. Serve via the NF3 STATIC S3 JSON path (a daily artifact is static by nature — do NOT add a request-time lakehouse read; E9.26b).
🔒 HONEST: report the match rate (overall + rookies separately — rookies are where the bridge is weakest AND where it matters most); an unmatched player is shown as unmatched, never silently omitted from a roster.
🔎 READINESS PASS (PM 2026-08-01) — verified against the real code; two findings sharpen the DO:
  (1) ⭐ **THE BRIDGE TARGET IS `gsis_id`, AND THE JOIN IS DIRECT — not a generic "external id" search.** Confirmed: our NFL projection + board key on **`gsis_id`** (`season_projection.py` sets `frame["gsis_id"]`; `export_draft_board_json.py`'s `player_id` == the P1A rookie `gsis_id`; the bio join reads `nflverse_players` on `gsis_id`). Sleeper's `/v1/players/nfl` carries `gsis_id` per row → **the bridge is a straight `sleeper.gsis_id == our gsis_id` join**, no fuzzy leg for the common case. ⇒ the name+pos+team FALLBACK is needed ONLY where one side LACKS a `gsis_id` — nflverse ships null/late `gsis_id` for ROOKIES/IDPs (the NF-D12 lesson), which is exactly the draft-relevant cohort → **report the fallback's match rate SEPARATELY for rookies** (that's where it's weakest and matters most), and the fallback must disclose, never silently drop.
  (2) ⭐ **THERE IS NO NFL-FANTASY DAILY-JOB SCHEDULER — decide the cadence mechanism, do NOT assume Dagster.** NFL fantasy is manual `run_*.py` + `export_draft_board_json.py --publish` (the MLB Dagster-on-the-box pipeline does NOT cover it). "Daily" in the spec is Sleeper's MAX-frequency GUIDANCE (fetch at most once/day), NOT a hard requirement to fire every day — the player file changes slowly. So the launch-correct answer is the CHEAPEST cadence that keeps names fresh: fetch + slim + upload the artifact as a STEP in the operator's existing export/publish workflow (or a lightweight cron / GitHub-Action if truly unattended), cached to S3 — do NOT build a new Dagster integration. Pick + state the mechanism in the handoff.
  (3) **THE BACKEND NAME-RESOLUTION READ MUST BE NARROW + MEMOIZED, not a per-request 5 MB fetch (the E9.26b wide-read landmine).** Slim the artifact to `gsis_id/sleeper_id → {name, pos, team}` (drop ~90% of Sleeper's fields) and load it ONCE (module-level memo in the Lambda), not per import. ⚠️ a resolution read returning `[]`/all-unmatched is SUSPECT — never render a whole roster as unmatched off an empty read (treat it as a failed load, not "no names exist").
  (4) **SCOPE IS SLEEPER-ONLY (confirmed): ESPN returns player names INLINE** (NF-C0), so this story does NOT touch the ESPN path — the ID→name bridge is a Sleeper-only problem. Don't re-plumb ESPN.
GATE/AC: a Sleeper player artifact (slimmed, cached ≤1×/day, cadence-mechanism stated) + a `gsis_id`-direct id bridge with a REPORTED match rate (overall AND rookies separately) + a name+pos+team fallback for the no-gsis_id cohort that discloses (never drops); imported rosters and draft picks render real names; the "IDs only" warning removed ONLY where names resolved; unmatched entries shown as unmatched; the backend read narrow+memoized; fast gate green. 🌿 GIT (do this FIRST, before touching any file): `git checkout dev && git pull && git checkout -b <story-branch>`; commit + push to THAT branch, hand back a PR → `dev`. ⛔ NEVER work on / commit to `dev`/`main` directly, ⛔ NEVER `git add -A`; re-check `git branch --show-current` before each commit (a parallel session shares the checkout); any `--publish`/`deploy.sh` is a POST-MERGE operator step. ▶ NF-C0d — ADMIN VISIBILITY: which league settings our users score that we DON'T project (the coverage-gap telemetry)   [App/backend · 🧭 SONNET · small · from the operator ask 2026-08-01]   ✅ DONE 2026-08-03 (PR #536 merged to `dev`; backend `deploy.sh` done; ⏳ `dev`→`main` = prod frontend deploy, LEFT FOR OPERATOR)
✅ **NF-C0d DONE 2026-08-03.** Shipped: `app/backend/services/fantasy_import_telemetry.py` (best-effort, non-raising S3 writer/reader — one JSON per import at `fantasy-import-telemetry/{platform}/{uuid}.json`, row `{platform, key, weight, verdict, season, imported_at}`; `aggregate_captured_terms` ranks by **occurrences × avg(|weight|)** — deliberately NOT × total(|weight|), which would double-count frequency); `POST /fantasy/import/telemetry` (entitlement-gated, always 202, write can never raise); `GET /admin/fantasy-import-telemetry` (admin-gated ranked list); frontend one-line disclosure + a fire-and-forget `recordCapturedTermTelemetry()` in `saveImported()` whose rejection is swallowed so telemetry can never fail an import; a "Captured Scoring Terms" `/admin` panel. 22 new tests; core (1275) + serving-ops (928) shards green, tsc clean; changelog line in the 2026-07-27 block. ⭐ Privacy is STRUCTURAL not policy — the Pydantic models have NO field for anything but platform/key/weight/verdict/season, so nothing identifying can reach the store even if a client tried. Only `verdict=="captured"` rows are sent/counted (a coverage-gap instrument, not a general telemetry pipe).
  🚩 **OPERATOR ACTIONS: (a) `dev`→`main` merge is the PROD FRONTEND DEPLOY (auto-deploys on push to `main`) — not done, left for you; (b) ⚠️ PURGE QA NOISE BEFORE TRUSTING THE RANKING — your Preview-testing imports wrote REAL rows into the same `fantasy-import-telemetry/` prefix the admin panel reads, and there is NO delete/purge endpoint → manually clean `s3://…/fantasy-import-telemetry/` (or tag which rows are your test imports) before NF-C0e reads the ranking, or it prioritizes off test noise.**
  📥 **NF-C0e DESIGN INPUTS (the telemetry now feeds NF-C0e's priority order — carry these as constraints):** (1) sample is THIN + beta-bounded (admin+fantasy_comp only) → read frequencies as DIRECTIONAL, not statistically strong, until import opens wider; (2) ESPN captured keys are RAW numeric ids in telemetry (the human label `unmapped_labels` e.g. "129@dst"→"Yards allowed tier (D/ST)" is NOT sent) → NF-C0e must cross-ref the ESPN adapter, or a follow-up adds the label to the row (non-PII, safe); (3) NO cross-platform merging — ranking groups strictly by `(platform,key)`, so a concept common across platforms under different names (Sleeper `st_ff` vs Yahoo's equiv) is SPLIT + under-counted → eyeball-merge semantically related rows, don't trust raw order; (4) NEGATIVE weights count at full magnitude (a penalty term ranks like an equal-magnitude positive) — intentional (both = board inaccuracy), but don't misread the top as "most-requested positive stats"; (5) SCOPE GAP — this is the platform-IMPORT save flow ONLY; NF-C0b manual hand-entered leagues record NOTHING here (the applied/derived/captured verdict is computed client-side in `resolveScoring` and the manual editor doesn't send it) → hand-typed leagues' captured rules don't exist anywhere yet; (6) admin panel has a 5-min `staleTime` — a just-done import won't show immediately when demoing. (Original prompt ↓.)
🎯 WHY: NF-C0's coverage machinery already classifies every imported term as applied / derived / **captured**, and a captured term is one the user's league really scores and our board really ignores. Today that verdict is shown to the USER and then thrown away — **nobody on our side can see which gaps are common**. So we cannot prioritise: is `yds_allow_*` in 2% of leagues or 60%? The first real import (a 12-team half-PPR league, 2026-08-01) had **20 captured terms**, which is either a big deal or a long-tail curiosity, and we currently have no way to tell which.
🔬 DO: (1) on a successful import, record an AGGREGATE row — `{platform, canonical_or_platform_key, weight, verdict, season, imported_at}` — to a small store (the users table's existing pattern, or an S3 append; **no new infra**, per NF-C0b's reasoning). ⭐ **AGGREGATE, NOT SURVEILLANCE: record the SETTING and its weight, never a user id, team names, or roster contents.** The question being answered is "which scoring rules do our users' leagues have that we can't honour" — that needs the rule, not the person. (2) an ADMIN surface (`/admin`, existing admin gate) ranking captured terms by **frequency × |weight|** — a term that appears in 40% of leagues at 6 points matters far more than one appearing once at 1 point. (3) feed that ranking straight into NF-C0e's priority order. ⚠️ **THE TELEMETRY WRITE ON IMPORT MUST BE BEST-EFFORT / NON-BLOCKING — an import must NEVER fail because the aggregate-telemetry write failed** (wrap it, log-on-failure, don't propagate; the import succeeding for the user is the serving-critical path, the telemetry is peripheral). ⚠️ the new admin read endpoint is on the `app/backend/` API Lambda (NO CD) → it ships via `deploy.sh` as a post-merge operator step; the response shape is additive.
🚨 APP-TARGET: `frontend/` admin surface + `app/backend/routers/admin.py`; ⛔ never Streamlit. Entitlement = the existing admin gate.
🔒 HONEST + PRIVACY: aggregate only; state in the import UI that we record which SETTINGS were unsupported (not who has them) so the product can close the gap — a one-line disclosure, not a buried policy change.
GATE/AC: captured-term telemetry recorded on import (aggregate, no user identifiers), an admin view ranked by frequency × |weight|, disclosed in the import UI; fast gate green. 🌿 GIT (do this FIRST, before touching any file): `git checkout dev && git pull && git checkout -b <story-branch>`; commit + push to THAT branch, hand back a PR → `dev`. ⛔ NEVER work on / commit to `dev`/`main` directly, ⛔ NEVER `git add -A`; re-check `git branch --show-current` before each commit (a parallel session shares the checkout); any `--publish`/`deploy.sh` is a POST-MERGE operator step.
